141500:      "content": "{\"ok\":true,\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/crates/ploke-rag/src/lib.rs\",\"exists\":true,\"byte_len\":7321,\"start_line\":null,\"end_line\":null,\"truncated\":false,\"content\":\"#![allow(unused_variables, unused_imports, dead_code)]\\n//! ploke-rag — Retrieval and context assembly for the PLOKE workspace\\n//!\\n//! This crate provides the \\\"R\\\" and \\\"A\\\" of RAG for the PLOKE workspace:\\n//! - Retrieval: sparse (BM25) via an in-memory actor and dense (HNSW) via `ploke-db`.\\n//! - Fusion: score normalization, weighted Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF), optional Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR).\\n//! - Context assembly: selecting, trimming, ordering and packaging snippets under a token budget.\\n//!\\n//! The design emphasizes deterministic behavior, strong observability, and strict error handling,\\n//! while integrating with the rest of the PLOKE system (database, IO manager, and embedding backends).\\n//!\\n//! Highlights\\n//! - Async BM25 client wrapper with timeouts, retries/backoff, and strict vs. lenient modes.\\n//! - Dense retrieval across all primary node types using [`ploke_db`]'s HNSW helpers.\\n//! - Pluggable fusion (RRF + MMR) and score normalization utilities.\\n//! - Context assembly with budgeting, deduplication, and stable ordering.\\n//! - Structured tracing for operability (suitable for tests and metrics-ready).\\n//!\\n//! Concurrency and actors\\n//! - BM25 is served by an actor (Tokio task) in `ploke-db` receiving `Bm25Cmd` over `mpsc` and\\n//!   replying via `oneshot` channels. All calls from this crate are enforced with client-side timeouts.\\n//! - Dense retrieval executes against `ploke-db` HNSW via synchronous calls wrapped in async fns.\\n//! - Snippet IO is delegated to `ploke-io`'s actor (if configured) and is used by reranking and\\n//!   by the context assembler.\\n//!\\n//! Error handling\\n//! - Channel send/recv failures map to [`RagError::Channel`].\\n//! - Database/actor errors map to [`RagError::Db`] while preserving inner messages.\\n//! - Embedding pipeline failures map to [`RagError::Embed`].\\n//! - Search state violations (e.g., strict BM25 while uninitialized) map to [`RagError::Search`].\\n//! - A conversion into the workspace error type is provided (`impl From<RagError> for ploke_error::Error`).\\n//!\\n//! Observability\\n//! - The BM25 client wrapper emits fields such as: `bm25_status`, `strict`, `attempts`,\\n//!   `timeout_ms`, `fallback_used`, `bm25_results`, and `dense_results`.\\n//! - Tests can opt-in to tracing; non-test binaries get a minimal, target-less fmt layer by default.\\n//!\\n//! Configuration\\n//! - See [`RagConfig`] to control BM25 timeouts/backoff/strict default, fusion settings,\\n//!   per-type dense search parameters, context assembly policy, tokenizer, and (optional) reranker.\\n//!\\n//! Quickstart\\n//! ```no_run\\n//! use std::sync::Arc;\\n//! use ploke_core::RetrievalScope;\\n//! use ploke_rag::{RagService, RetrievalStrategy, TokenBudget};\\n//! use ploke_embed::runtime::EmbeddingRuntime;\\n//! use ploke_db::{Database};\\n//!\\n//! # async fn doc_example(mut db: Arc<Database>, embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\\n//! // Construct the service (BM25 actor is started internally).\\n//! let rag = RagService::new(db.clone(), embedder.clone())?;\\n//!\\n//! // Sparse-only (lenient): falls back to dense if BM25 is unready/empty.\\n//! let bm25_hits = rag.search_bm25(\\\"how to implement graph nodes\\\", 15, RetrievalScope::LoadedWorkspace).await?;\\n//!\\n//! // Hybrid: concurrent BM25 + dense, fused via weighted RRF.\\n//! let fused = rag.hybrid_search(\\\"module visibility rules\\\", 15, RetrievalScope::LoadedWorkspace).await?;\\n//!\\n//! // Assemble a context for downstream prompting.\\n//! let budget = TokenBudget { max_total: 1024, per_file_max: 512, per_part_max: 256 };\\n//! let ctx = rag.get_context(\\n//!     \\\"module visibility rules\\\",\\n//!     12,\\n//!     &budget,\\n//!     &RetrievalStrategy::Hybrid { rrf: Default::default(), mmr: None },\\n//!     RetrievalScope::LoadedWorkspace,\\n//! ).await?;\\n//! # Ok(()) }\\n//! ```\\n//!\\n//! BM25 lifecycle and persistence\\n//! - This crate re-exports [`Bm25Status`] and provides thin call-throughs for `save` and `load`.\\n//!   Current persistence is a lightweight sidecar (version + doc_count) and `load` triggers a rebuild\\n//!   from the DB in `ploke-db`. Keep API usage stable to benefit from future serialization improvements.\\n//!\\n//! Algorithms\\n//! - RRF: [`rrf_fuse`] implements weighted reciprocal rank fusion with stable UUID tie-breaking.\\n//! - Score normalization: [`normalize_scores`] provides min-max, z-score, and logistic transforms.\\n//! - MMR: [`mmr_select`] performs diversity-aware selection using cosine similarity on normalized vectors.\\n//!\\n//! Threading and performance\\n//! - `RagService` holds `Arc`s to the database handle and embedding processor, and an `mpsc` sender to BM25.\\n//!   Clone or share it behind `Arc` in your application services.\\n//! - BM25 RPCs are local and fast; a client-side timeout guards unexpected stalls (default 250ms) with\\n//!   up to two status/backoff retries in lenient mode.\\n//!\\n//! Stability and versioning\\n//! - Public APIs follow SemVer. The default behavior of existing methods is preserved; strict and persistence\\n//!   features are opt-in. See `crates/ploke-rag/IMPLEMENTATION_NOTES.md` and `plans.md` for cross-crate design.\\n//!\\n//! Re-exports\\n//! - [`Bm25Status`], [`RrfConfig`], [`MmrConfig`], [`Similarity`], [`ScoreNorm`],\\n//!   [`normalize_scores`], [`rrf_fuse`], [`mmr_select`], and context assembly types.\\n//!\\n//! Tips\\n//! - In tests, initialize tracing with your preferred subscriber; the library avoids double-registration.\\n//! - Provide a custom [`TokenCounter`] to align budgeting with your downstream LLM tokenizer.\\n//!\\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\\nuse std::sync::Arc;\\n// use tracing_subscriber::{prelude::__tracing_subscriber_SubscriberExt, util::SubscriberInitExt};\\n\\n#[cfg(not(test))]\\nstatic TRACER_INIT: std::sync::Once = std::sync::Once::new();\\n\\n#[cfg(test)]\\nstatic _TRACE_MARKER: () = ();\\n\\n// #[cfg(not(test))]\\n// fn ensure_tracer_initialized() {\\n//     TRACER_INIT.call_once(|| {\\n//         let fmt_layer = tracing_subscriber::fmt::layer().with_target(false);\\n//         let _ = tracing_subscriber::registry().with(fmt_layer).try_init();\\n//     });\\n// }\\n\\n#[cfg(test)]\\nfn ensure_tracer_initialized() {\\n    // Tests configure tracing themselves; no-op to avoid interfering with test harness.\\n}\\n\\nuse ploke_core::{\\n    EmbeddingData, RetrievalScope,\\n    rag_types::{TypeContextInfo, TypeContextKind},\\n};\\nuse ploke_db::{\\n    Database, DbError, NodeType, SimilarArgs, TypeContextOptions, TypeContextRelation,\\n    TypeContextSeed, TypedEmbedData,\\n    bm25_index::bm25_service::{self, Bm25Cmd},\\n    search_similar_args,\\n};\\nuse ploke_embed::indexer::{EmbeddingProcessor, IndexerTask};\\nuse thiserror::Error;\\nuse tokio::sync::{mpsc, oneshot};\\nuse tokio::time::{Duration, sleep, timeout};\\nuse tracing::{debug, instrument};\\nuse uuid::Uuid;\\n\\npub mod error;\\npub mod fusion;\\npub use error::RagError;\\npub use fusion::{\\n    MmrConfig, RrfConfig, ScoreNorm, Similarity, mmr_select, normalize_scores, rrf_fuse,\\n};\\npub mod context;\\npub use context::{\\n    ApproxCharTokenizer, AssemblyPolicy, Ordering, TokenBudget, TokenCounter, assemble_context,\\n    assemble_context_with_type_context,\\n};\\npub mod core;\\npub use core::{\\n    NoopReranker, RagConfig, RagService, Reranker, RetrievalStrategy, TypeContextConfig,\\n};\\npub use ploke_db::bm25_index::bm25_service::Bm25Status;\\n\\nconst BM25_TIMEOUT_MS: u64 = 250;\\nconst BM25_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS: [u64; 2] = [50, 100];\\n\",\"file_hash\":[81,78,198,14,143,70,69,183,206,169,253,144,252,12,57,236,173,45,53,115,49,250,210,85,65,99,169,152,98,195,120,86]}",

141576:      "content": "{\"byte_len\":35226,\"content\":\"#![allow(missing_docs)]\\n//! Core orchestration: `RagService` and configuration.\\n//!\\n//! [`RagService`] is the primary entry point for performing sparse, dense, and hybrid retrieval,\\n//! as well as assembling a token-budgeted context. It wraps the BM25 actor with timeouts, optional\\n//! retries/backoff (lenient mode), and exposes strict variants and persistence hooks.\\n//!\\n//! Configure behavior via [`RagConfig`], including fusion defaults, dense search parameters, and\\n//! context assembly policy. For diversity or learning-to-rank experiments, plug in a custom [`Reranker`].\\nmod unit_tests;\\nuse super::*;\\nuse ploke_core::rag_types::AssembledContext;\\nuse ploke_embed::indexer::EmbeddingProcessor;\\nuse ploke_embed::runtime::EmbeddingRuntime;\\nuse ploke_io::IoManagerHandle;\\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nuse std::collections::HashSet;\\nuse std::future::Future;\\nuse std::pin::Pin;\\nuse std::sync::Arc;\\nuse tracing::trace;\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub enum RetrievalStrategy {\\n    Dense,\\n    Sparse {\\n        strict: Option<bool>,\\n    },\\n    Hybrid {\\n        rrf: RrfConfig,\\n        mmr: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    },\\n}\\nimpl Default for RetrievalStrategy {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self::Hybrid {\\n            rrf: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr: None,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct SearchParams {\\n    pub ef: usize,\\n    pub radius: f64,\\n    pub max_hits: usize,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for SearchParams {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            ef: 10,\\n            radius: 10.0,\\n            max_hits: 0,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\\npub struct RagConfig {\\n    pub bm25_timeout_ms: u64,\\n    pub bm25_retry_backoff_ms: Vec<u64>,\\n    pub strict_bm25_by_default: bool,\\n    pub rrf_default: RrfConfig,\\n    pub mmr_default: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    pub score_norm: ScoreNorm,\\n    pub search_per_type: HashMap<NodeType, SearchParams>,\\n    pub assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy,\\n    pub token_counter: Arc<dyn TokenCounter>,\\n    pub reranker: Option<Arc<dyn Reranker>>,\\n    pub type_context: TypeContextConfig,\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct TypeContextConfig {\\n    pub enabled: bool,\\n    pub max_seed_hits: usize,\\n    pub max_expanded_hits: usize,\\n    pub score_factor: f32,\\n    pub options: TypeContextOptions,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for TypeContextConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            enabled: cfg!(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\"),\\n            max_seed_hits: 12,\\n            max_expanded_hits: 48,\\n            score_factor: 0.6,\\n            options: TypeContextOptions {\\n                max_distance: 4,\\n                ..TypeContextOptions::default()\\n            },\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for RagConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            bm25_timeout_ms: crate::BM25_TIMEOUT_MS,\\n            bm25_retry_backoff_ms: crate::BM25_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS.to_vec(),\\n            strict_bm25_by_default: false,\\n            rrf_default: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr_default: None,\\n            score_norm: ScoreNorm::default(),\\n            search_per_type: HashMap::new(),\\n            assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy::default(),\\n            token_counter: Arc::new(crate::context::ApproxCharTokenizer),\\n            reranker: None,\\n            type_context: TypeContextConfig::default(),\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagConfig {\\n    pub fn params_for(&self, ty: NodeType) -> SearchParams {\\n        self.search_per_type.get(&ty).copied().unwrap_or_default()\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// Optional async reranker for candidate reordering using snippet texts.\\n#[allow(clippy::type_complexity)]\\npub trait Reranker: Send + Sync + std::fmt::Debug {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>>;\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Default)]\\npub struct NoopReranker;\\n\\nimpl Reranker for NoopReranker {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        _query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>> {\\n        Box::pin(async move { Ok(candidates.into_iter().map(|(id, _)| (id, 1.0)).collect()) })\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nfn type_context_kind(relation: TypeContextRelation) -> TypeContextKind {\\n    match relation {\\n        TypeContextRelation::SameResolvedType => TypeContextKind::SameResolvedType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::UsesTypeNested => TypeContextKind::UsesTypeNested,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TypeDefinitionImpact => TypeContextKind::TypeDefinitionImpact,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplOfTrait => TypeContextKind::ImplOfTrait,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplSelfType => TypeContextKind::ImplSelfType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::AliasExpansion => TypeContextKind::AliasExpansion,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TraitBound => TypeContextKind::TraitBound,\\n        TypeContextRelation::IteratorSurface => TypeContextKind::IteratorSurface,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ConstGenericAlias => TypeContextKind::ConstGenericAlias,\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// RAG orchestration service.\\n///\\n/// This orchestrates hybrid search by combining:\\n/// - BM25 sparse search served by an in-memory actor\\n/// - Dense vector search served by the HNSW index in the database\\n///\\n/// Notes:\\n/// - BM25 search will gracefully fall back to dense search if the BM25 index is empty,\\n///   ensuring callers do not receive empty results due to indexing lag.\\n/// - Use `hybrid_search` to fuse the results via RRF for robust retrieval.\\n///\\n/// See crate tests for end-to-end examples using a fixture database.\\n#[derive(Debug)]\\npub struct RagService {\\n    db: Arc<Database>,\\n    dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n    bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n    cfg: RagConfig,\\n    io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    type_context_degraded: bool,\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagService {\\n    fn assemble(\\n        db: Arc<Database>,\\n        dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n        bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n        mut cfg: RagConfig,\\n        io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    ) -> Result<Self, RagError> {\\n        let type_context_degraded = Self::apply_type_context_gate(&db, &mut cfg)?;\\n        Ok(Self {\\n            db,\\n            dense_embedder,\\n            bm_embedder,\\n            cfg,\\n            io,\\n            type_context_degraded,\\n        })... Perform a hybrid search (BM25 + dense).\\n    ///\\n    /// Strict mode: if the dense search fails, propagate an error (do not silently fall back).\\n    /// Runs BM25 and dense searches concurrently, fuses results using Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF),\\n    /// and returns the top_k results ordered by fused score (higher = better).\\n    #[instrument(skip(self, query), fields(query_len = %query.len(), top_k = top_k))]\\n    pub async fn hybrid_search(\\n        &self,\\n        query: &str,\\n        top_k: usize,\\n        scope: RetrievalScope,\\n    ) -> Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError> {\\n        // Kick off both searches concurrently.\\n        let bm25_fut = self.search_bm25(query, top_k, scope);\\n        let dense_fut = self.search(query, top_k, scope);\\n\\n        let (bm25_res, dense_res) = tokio::join!(bm25_fut, dense_fut);\\n\\n        // Propagate BM25 errors as-is.\\n        let bm25_list = bm25_res?;\\n\\n        // Dense errors are mapped to RagError::Embed since we're in strict mode.\\n        let dense_list =\\n            dense_\",\"type_context\":null},{\"id\":\"387532ae-a2a8-5244-9e4d-06d7b4cca613\",\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/crates/ploke-rag/src/core/unit_tests.rs\",\"canon_path\":\"crate::core::unit_tests::test_hybrid_search\",\"snippet\":\"#[tokio::test]\\n Search with a query string, returning top-k results as ScoredDocument<Uuid>\\n    pub fn search(\\n        &self,\\n        query: &str,\\n        top_k: usize,\\n        scope: RetrievalScope,\\n    ) -> Vec<bm25::ScoredDocument<Uuid>> {\\n        let qemb = self.embedder.embed(query);\\n        tracing::debug!(\\\"qemb: {qemb:?}\\\");\\n        let mut matches = self.scorer.matches(&qemb);\\n        tracing::trace!(\\\"matches: {matches:?}\\\");\\n        if let Some(namespace) = scope.namespace_filter() {\\n            matches.retain(|doc| {\\n                self.staged_meta\\n                    .get(&doc.id)\\n                    .is_some_and(|meta| meta.namespace == namespace)\\n            });\\n        }\\n        if matches.len() > top_k {\\n            matches.truncate(top_k);\\n        }\\n        matches\\n    }\",\"type_context\":null},{\"id\":\"6297a6a2-7c64-57b1-a925-aac67d039294\",\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/crates/ploke-llm/src/manager/commands.rs\",\"canon_path\":\"crate::manager::commands::search\",\"snippet\":\"mod Perform a dense search using the HNSW index in the database.\\n    /// Returns a Vec of (snippet_id, score) pairs sorted by relevance.\\n    #[instrument(skip(self, query), fields(query_len = %query.len(), top_k = top_k))]\\n    pub async fn search(\\n        &self,\\n        query: &str,\\n        top_k: usize,\\n        scope: RetrievalScope,\\n    ) -> Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, ploke_error::Error> {\\n        // Generate embedding for the query\\n        let embeddings = self\\n            .dense_embedder\\n            .generate_embeddings(vec![query.to_string()])\\n            .await?;\\n\\n        let query_embedding = embeddings\\n            .into_iter()\\n            .next()\\n            .ok_or_else(|| RagError::Embed(\\\"failed to generate query embedding\\\".to_string()))?;\\n\\n        // Collect results from all node types including methods; pre-allocate to avoid reallocations\\n        let node_types = NodeType::primary_and_assoc_nodes();\\n        let mut all_results: Vec<(Uuid, f32)> = Vec::with_capacity(node_types.len() * top_k);\\n\\n       \",\"type_context\":null},{\"id\":\"b2658184-114c-5c65-a890-8013d1bb8cef\",\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/crates/ploke-records/src/run_profile.rs\",\"canon_path\":\"crate::run_profile::Search\",\"snippet\":\"#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]\\npub struct Search {\\n    pub max_generations: u32,\\n    pub max_total_nodes: u32,\\n    pub children: ChildBudgetRecord,\\n    pub schedule: ChildScheduleModeRecord,\\n    pub stop_on_first_keep: bool,\\n    pub require_keep_for_continuation: bool,\\n    pub explore_from_rejected: bool,\\n}\",\"type_context\":null},{\"id\":\"00cdd0e7-2000-5b7c-a5b1-da095ede1e12\",\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/crates/ploke-rag/src/core/mod.rs\",\"canon_path\":\"crate::core::search_bm25\",\"snippet\":\"/// Execute a BM25 search against the in-memory sparse index.\\n    ///\\n    /// Lenient mode: if the BM25 index is not ready or empty, this method may fall back to dense search.\\n    /// Returns a Vec of (document_id, score) pairs sorted by relevance.\\n    #[instrument(skip(self, query), fields(query_len = %query.len(), top_k = top_k, strict = false, timeout_ms = BM25_TIMEOUT_MS))]\\n    pub async fn search_bm25(\\n        &self,\\n        query: &str,\\n        top_k: usize,\\n        scope: RetrievalScope,\\n    ) -> Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError> {\\n        let mut attempts: usize = 0;\\n\\n        loop {\\n            attempts += 1;\\n\\n            let status_opt = match self.bm25_status().await {\\n                Ok(s) => Some(s),\\n                Err(e) => {\\n                    debug!(\\\"bm25_status check failed: {:?}\\\", e);\\n                    None\\n                }\\n            };\\n\\n            let (tx, rx) = oneshot::channel();\\n            self.bm_embedder\\n                .send(Bm25Cmd::Search {\\n                    query: query.\",\"type_context\":null},{\"id\":\"0c4f6b46-cdab-5a7a-bb80-5070beece545\",\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/crates/ploke-db/src/index/hnsw.rs\",\"canon_path\":\"crate::index::hnsw::search_similar\",\"snippet\":\"#[instrument(skip_all, fields(query_result))]\\npub fn search_similar(\\n    db: &Database,\\n    vector_query: Vec<f32>,\\n    k: usize,\\n    ef: usize,\\n    scope: RetrievalScope,\\n    ty: NodeType,\\n) -> Result<TypedEmbedData, ploke_error::Error> {\\n    // TODO:active-embedding-set 2025-12-15\\n    // update the active embedding set functions to correctly use Arc<RwLock<>> within these\\n    // functions.\\n    let active_embedding_set = db.with_active_set(|set| set.clone())?;\\n    db.search_similar_for_set(\\n        &active_embedding_set,\\n        ty,\\n        scope,\\n        vector_query,\\n        k,\\n        ef,\\n        100,\\n        None,\\n    )\\n    .map(|res| res.typed_data)\\n}\",\"type_context\":null},{\"id\":\"27476cd1-9373-5444-9070-1ff055f766f6\",\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/crates/ploke-rag/src/core/mod.rs\",\"canon_path\":\"crate::core::SearchParams\",\"snippet\":\"#[derive(Debug, search_terms(input: &str) -> BTreeSet<String> {\\n    input\\n        .split(|ch: char| !ch.is_alphanumeric() && ch != '!' && ch != '_')\\n        .filter(|term| !term.is_empty())\\n        .map(|term| term.to_ascii_lowercase())\\n        .collect()\\n}\",\"type_context\":null},{\"id\":\"3a669117-4d95-51bc-a69e-60f1f2eec24b\",\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/crates/ploke-rag/src/core/unit_tests.rs\",\"canon_path\":\"crate::core::unit_tests::test_search\",\"snippet\":\"#[tokio::test]\\n    async fn test_search() -> Result<(), Error> {\\n        init_tracing_once();\\n\\n        let search_term = \\\"use_all_const_static\\\";\\n\\n        let rag = &DEFAULT_TEST_RAG;\\n        let db = &DEFAULT_TEST_RAG.db;\\n\\n        let search_res: Vec<(Uuid, f32)> =\\n            rag.search(search_term, 15, LOADED_WORKSPACE_SCOPE).await?;\\n        assert!(\\n            !search_res.is_empty(),\\n            \\\"Dense search returned no results for '{}'\\\",\\n            search_term\\n        );\\n\\n        let ordered_node_ids: Vec<Uuid> = search_res.iter().map(|(id, _score)| *id).collect();\\n        fetch_and_assert_snippet(db, ordered_node_ids, search_term).await?;\\n        Ok(())\\n    }\",\"type_context\":null},{\"id\":\"72fafdec-a6a7-5bc4-9152-ef37790aa1fb\",\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/xtask/src/commands/pipeline.rs\",\"canon_path\":\"crate::commands::pipeline::search_text\",\"snippet\":\"fn search_text(&self) -> String {\\n        [\\n            Some(self.kind.as_str()),\\n            self.id.as_deref(),\\n            self.pipeline.as_deref(),\\n            self.status.as_deref(),\\n            self.owner_area.as_deref(),\\n            self.summary.as_deref(),\\n            self.stage.as_deref(),\\n            self.path.as_deref(),\\n            self.symbol.as_deref(),\\n            self.role.as_deref(),\\n        ]\\n        .into_iter()\\n        .flatten()\\n        .chain(\\n            self.docs\\n                .iter()\\n                .chain(self.supporting_docs.iter())\\n                .chain(self.tests.iter())\\n                .chain(self.bugs.iter())\\n                .map(String::as_str),\\n        )\\n        .join(\\\" \\\")\\n        .to_lowercase()\\n    }\",\"type_context\":null}]}",

141648:      "content": "{\"byte_len\":35226,\"content\":\"#![allow(missing_docs)]\\n//! Core orchestration: `RagService` and configuration.\\n//!\\n//! [`RagService`] is the primary entry point for performing sparse, dense, and hybrid retrieval,\\n//! as well as assembling a token-budgeted context. It wraps the BM25 actor with timeouts, optional\\n//! retries/backoff (lenient mode), and exposes strict variants and persistence hooks.\\n//!\\n//! Configure behavior via [`RagConfig`], including fusion defaults, dense search parameters, and\\n//! context assembly policy. For diversity or learning-to-rank experiments, plug in a custom [`Reranker`].\\nmod unit_tests;\\nuse super::*;\\nuse ploke_core::rag_types::AssembledContext;\\nuse ploke_embed::indexer::EmbeddingProcessor;\\nuse ploke_embed::runtime::EmbeddingRuntime;\\nuse ploke_io::IoManagerHandle;\\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nuse std::collections::HashSet;\\nuse std::future::Future;\\nuse std::pin::Pin;\\nuse std::sync::Arc;\\nuse tracing::trace;\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub enum RetrievalStrategy {\\n    Dense,\\n    Sparse {\\n        strict: Option<bool>,\\n    },\\n    Hybrid {\\n        rrf: RrfConfig,\\n        mmr: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    },\\n}\\nimpl Default for RetrievalStrategy {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self::Hybrid {\\n            rrf: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr: None,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct SearchParams {\\n    pub ef: usize,\\n    pub radius: f64,\\n    pub max_hits: usize,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for SearchParams {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            ef: 10,\\n            radius: 10.0,\\n            max_hits: 0,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\\npub struct RagConfig {\\n    pub bm25_timeout_ms: u64,\\n    pub bm25_retry_backoff_ms: Vec<u64>,\\n    pub strict_bm25_by_default: bool,\\n    pub rrf_default: RrfConfig,\\n    pub mmr_default: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    pub score_norm: ScoreNorm,\\n    pub search_per_type: HashMap<NodeType, SearchParams>,\\n    pub assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy,\\n    pub token_counter: Arc<dyn TokenCounter>,\\n    pub reranker: Option<Arc<dyn Reranker>>,\\n    pub type_context: TypeContextConfig,\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct TypeContextConfig {\\n    pub enabled: bool,\\n    pub max_seed_hits: usize,\\n    pub max_expanded_hits: usize,\\n    pub score_factor: f32,\\n    pub options: TypeContextOptions,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for TypeContextConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            enabled: cfg!(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\"),\\n            max_seed_hits: 12,\\n            max_expanded_hits: 48,\\n            score_factor: 0.6,\\n            options: TypeContextOptions {\\n                max_distance: 4,\\n                ..TypeContextOptions::default()\\n            },\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for RagConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            bm25_timeout_ms: crate::BM25_TIMEOUT_MS,\\n            bm25_retry_backoff_ms: crate::BM25_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS.to_vec(),\\n            strict_bm25_by_default: false,\\n            rrf_default: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr_default: None,\\n            score_norm: ScoreNorm::default(),\\n            search_per_type: HashMap::new(),\\n            assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy::default(),\\n            token_counter: Arc::new(crate::context::ApproxCharTokenizer),\\n            reranker: None,\\n            type_context: TypeContextConfig::default(),\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagConfig {\\n    pub fn params_for(&self, ty: NodeType) -> SearchParams {\\n        self.search_per_type.get(&ty).copied().unwrap_or_default()\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// Optional async reranker for candidate reordering using snippet texts.\\n#[allow(clippy::type_complexity)]\\npub trait Reranker: Send + Sync + std::fmt::Debug {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>>;\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Default)]\\npub struct NoopReranker;\\n\\nimpl Reranker for NoopReranker {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        _query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>> {\\n        Box::pin(async move { Ok(candidates.into_iter().map(|(id, _)| (id, 1.0)).collect()) })\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nfn type_context_kind(relation: TypeContextRelation) -> TypeContextKind {\\n    match relation {\\n        TypeContextRelation::SameResolvedType => TypeContextKind::SameResolvedType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::UsesTypeNested => TypeContextKind::UsesTypeNested,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TypeDefinitionImpact => TypeContextKind::TypeDefinitionImpact,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplOfTrait => TypeContextKind::ImplOfTrait,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplSelfType => TypeContextKind::ImplSelfType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::AliasExpansion => TypeContextKind::AliasExpansion,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TraitBound => TypeContextKind::TraitBound,\\n        TypeContextRelation::IteratorSurface => TypeContextKind::IteratorSurface,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ConstGenericAlias => TypeContextKind::ConstGenericAlias,\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// RAG orchestration service.\\n///\\n/// This orchestrates hybrid search by combining:\\n/// - BM25 sparse search served by an in-memory actor\\n/// - Dense vector search served by the HNSW index in the database\\n///\\n/// Notes:\\n/// - BM25 search will gracefully fall back to dense search if the BM25 index is empty,\\n///   ensuring callers do not receive empty results due to indexing lag.\\n/// - Use `hybrid_search` to fuse the results via RRF for robust retrieval.\\n///\\n/// See crate tests for end-to-end examples using a fixture database.\\n#[derive(Debug)]\\npub struct RagService {\\n    db: Arc<Database>,\\n    dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n    bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n    cfg: RagConfig,\\n    io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    type_context_degraded: bool,\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagService {\\n    fn assemble(\\n        db: Arc<Database>,\\n        dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n        bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n        mut cfg: RagConfig,\\n        io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    ) -> Result<Self, RagError> {\\n        let type_context_degraded = Self::apply_type_context_gate(&db, &mut cfg)?;\\n        Ok(Self {\\n            db,\\n            dense_embedder,\\n            bm_embedder,\\n            cfg,\\n            io,\\n            type_context_degraded,\\n        })... 141900:      "content": "{\"byte_len\":35133,\"content\":\"#![allow(missing_docs)]\\n//! Core orchestration: `RagService` and configuration.\\n//!\\n//! [`RagService`] is the primary entry point for performing sparse, dense, and hybrid retrieval,\\n//! as well as assembling a token-budgeted context. It wraps the BM25 actor with timeouts, optional\\n//! retries/backoff (lenient mode), and exposes strict variants and persistence hooks.\\n//!\\n//! Configure behavior via [`RagConfig`], including fusion defaults, dense search parameters, and\\n//! context assembly policy. For diversity or learning-to-rank experiments, plug in a custom [`Reranker`].\\nmod unit_tests;\\nuse super::*;\\nuse ploke_core::rag_types::AssembledContext;\\nuse ploke_embed::indexer::EmbeddingProcessor;\\nuse ploke_embed::runtime::EmbeddingRuntime;\\nuse ploke_io::IoManagerHandle;\\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nuse std::collections::HashSet;\\nuse std::future::Future;\\nuse std::pin::Pin;\\nuse std::sync::Arc;\\nuse tracing::trace;\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub enum RetrievalStrategy {\\n    Dense,\\n    Sparse {\\n        strict: Option<bool>,\\n    },\\n    Hybrid {\\n        rrf: RrfConfig,\\n        mmr: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    },\\n}\\nimpl Default for RetrievalStrategy {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self::Hybrid {\\n            rrf: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr: None,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct SearchParams {\\n    pub ef: usize,\\n    pub radius: f64,\\n    pub max_hits: usize,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for SearchParams {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            ef: 10,\\n            radius: 10.0,\\n            max_hits: 0,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\\npub struct RagConfig {\\n    pub bm25_timeout_ms: u64,\\n    pub bm25_retry_backoff_ms: Vec<u64>,\\n    pub strict_bm25_by_default: bool,\\n    pub rrf_default: RrfConfig,\\n    pub mmr_default: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    pub score_norm: ScoreNorm,\\n    pub search_per_type: HashMap<NodeType, SearchParams>,\\n    pub assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy,\\n    pub token_counter: Arc<dyn TokenCounter>,\\n    pub reranker: Option<Arc<dyn Reranker>>,\\n    pub type_context: TypeContextConfig,\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct TypeContextConfig {\\n    pub enabled: bool,\\n    pub max_seed_hits: usize,\\n    pub max_expanded_hits: usize,\\n    pub score_factor: f32,\\n    pub options: TypeContextOptions,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for TypeContextConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            enabled: cfg!(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\"),\\n            max_seed_hits: 12,\\n            max_expanded_hits: 48,\\n            score_factor: 0.6,\\n            options: TypeContextOptions {\\n                max_distance: 4,\\n                ..TypeContextOptions::default()\\n            },\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for RagConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            bm25_timeout_ms: crate::BM25_TIMEOUT_MS,\\n            bm25_retry_backoff_ms: crate::BM25_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS.to_vec(),\\n            strict_bm25_by_default: false,\\n            rrf_default: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr_default: None,\\n            score_norm: ScoreNorm::default(),\\n            search_per_type: HashMap::new(),\\n            assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy::default(),\\n            token_counter: Arc::new(crate::context::ApproxCharTokenizer),\\n            reranker: None,\\n            type_context: TypeContextConfig::default(),\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagConfig {\\n    pub fn params_for(&self, ty: NodeType) -> SearchParams {\\n        self.search_per_type.get(&ty).copied().unwrap_or_default()\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// Optional async reranker for candidate reordering using snippet texts.\\n#[allow(clippy::type_complexity)]\\npub trait Reranker: Send + Sync + std::fmt::Debug {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>>;\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Default)]\\npub struct NoopReranker;\\n\\nimpl Reranker for NoopReranker {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        _query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>> {\\n        Box::pin(async move { Ok(candidates.into_iter().map(|(id, _)| (id, 1.0)).collect()) })\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nfn type_context_kind(relation: TypeContextRelation) -> TypeContextKind {\\n    match relation {\\n        TypeContextRelation::SameResolvedType => TypeContextKind::SameResolvedType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::UsesTypeNested => TypeContextKind::UsesTypeNested,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TypeDefinitionImpact => TypeContextKind::TypeDefinitionImpact,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplOfTrait => TypeContextKind::ImplOfTrait,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplSelfType => TypeContextKind::ImplSelfType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::AliasExpansion => TypeContextKind::AliasExpansion,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TraitBound => TypeContextKind::TraitBound,\\n        TypeContextRelation::IteratorSurface => TypeContextKind::IteratorSurface,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ConstGenericAlias => TypeContextKind::ConstGenericAlias,\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// RAG orchestration service.\\n///\\n/// This orchestrates hybrid search by combining:\\n/// - BM25 sparse search served by an in-memory actor\\n/// - Dense vector search served by the HNSW index in the database\\n///\\n/// Notes:\\n/// - BM25 search will gracefully fall back to dense search if the BM25 index is empty,\\n///   ensuring callers do not receive empty results due to indexing lag.\\n/// - Use `hybrid_search` to fuse the results via RRF for robust retrieval.\\n///\\n/// See crate tests for end-to-end examples using a fixture database.\\n#[derive(Debug)]\\npub struct RagService {\\n    db: Arc<Database>,\\n    dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n    bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n    cfg: RagConfig,\\n    io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    type_context_degraded: bool,\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagService {\\n    fn assemble(\\n        db: Arc<Database>,\\n        dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n        bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n        mut cfg: RagConfig,\\n        io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    ) -> Result<Self, RagError> {\\n        let type_context_degraded = Self::apply_type_context_gate(&db, &mut cfg)?;\\n        Ok(Self {\\n            db,\\n            dense_embedder,\\n            bm_embedder,\\n            cfg,\\n            io,\\n            type_context_degraded,\\n        })... [truncated for LLM replay after 200 lines]\",\"end_line\":null,\"exists\":true,\"file_hash\":[3,225,39,9,87,171,21,59,45,239,60,106,148,35,33,124,72,9,183,6,30,13,150,107,95,41,53,154,203,249,220,240],\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/crates/ploke-rag/src/core/mod.rs\",\"llm_replay_max_lines\":200,\"llm_replay_truncated\":true,\"ok\":true,\"start_line\":null,\"truncated\":true}",

hybrid search by combining:\\n/// - BM25 sparse search served by an in-memory actor\\n/// - Dense vector search served by the HNSW index in the database\\n///\\n/// Notes:\\n/// - BM25 search will 141948:      "content": "{\"byte_len\":35133,\"content\":\"#![allow(missing_docs)]\\n//! Core orchestration: `RagService` and configuration.\\n//!\\n//! [`RagService`] is the primary entry point for performing sparse, dense, and hybrid retrieval,\\n//! as well as assembling a token-budgeted context. It wraps the BM25 actor with timeouts, optional\\n//! retries/backoff (lenient mode), and exposes strict variants and persistence hooks.\\n//!\\n//! Configure behavior via [`RagConfig`], including fusion defaults, dense search parameters, and\\n//! context assembly policy. For diversity or learning-to-rank experiments, plug in a custom [`Reranker`].\\nmod unit_tests;\\nuse super::*;\\nuse ploke_core::rag_types::AssembledContext;\\nuse ploke_embed::indexer::EmbeddingProcessor;\\nuse ploke_embed::runtime::EmbeddingRuntime;\\nuse ploke_io::IoManagerHandle;\\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nuse std::collections::HashSet;\\nuse std::future::Future;\\nuse std::pin::Pin;\\nuse std::sync::Arc;\\nuse tracing::trace;\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub enum RetrievalStrategy {\\n    Dense,\\n    Sparse {\\n        strict: Option<bool>,\\n    },\\n    Hybrid {\\n        rrf: RrfConfig,\\n        mmr: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    },\\n}\\nimpl Default for RetrievalStrategy {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self::Hybrid {\\n            rrf: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr: None,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct SearchParams {\\n    pub ef: usize,\\n    pub radius: f64,\\n    pub max_hits: usize,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for SearchParams {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            ef: 10,\\n            radius: 10.0,\\n            max_hits: 0,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\\npub struct RagConfig {\\n    pub bm25_timeout_ms: u64,\\n    pub bm25_retry_backoff_ms: Vec<u64>,\\n    pub strict_bm25_by_default: bool,\\n    pub rrf_default: RrfConfig,\\n    pub mmr_default: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    pub score_norm: ScoreNorm,\\n    pub search_per_type: HashMap<NodeType, SearchParams>,\\n    pub assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy,\\n    pub token_counter: Arc<dyn TokenCounter>,\\n    pub reranker: Option<Arc<dyn Reranker>>,\\n    pub type_context: TypeContextConfig,\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct TypeContextConfig {\\n    pub enabled: bool,\\n    pub max_seed_hits: usize,\\n    pub max_expanded_hits: usize,\\n    pub score_factor: f32,\\n    pub options: TypeContextOptions,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for TypeContextConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            enabled: cfg!(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\"),\\n            max_seed_hits: 12,\\n            max_expanded_hits: 48,\\n            score_factor: 0.6,\\n            options: TypeContextOptions {\\n                max_distance: 4,\\n                ..TypeContextOptions::default()\\n            },\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for RagConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            bm25_timeout_ms: crate::BM25_TIMEOUT_MS,\\n            bm25_retry_backoff_ms: crate::BM25_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS.to_vec(),\\n            strict_bm25_by_default: false,\\n            rrf_default: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr_default: None,\\n            score_norm: ScoreNorm::default(),\\n            search_per_type: HashMap::new(),\\n            assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy::default(),\\n            token_counter: Arc::new(crate::context::ApproxCharTokenizer),\\n            reranker: None,\\n            type_context: TypeContextConfig::default(),\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagConfig {\\n    pub fn params_for(&self, ty: NodeType) -> SearchParams {\\n        self.search_per_type.get(&ty).copied().unwrap_or_default()\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// Optional async reranker for candidate reordering using snippet texts.\\n#[allow(clippy::type_complexity)]\\npub trait Reranker: Send + Sync + std::fmt::Debug {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>>;\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Default)]\\npub struct NoopReranker;\\n\\nimpl Reranker for NoopReranker {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        _query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>> {\\n        Box::pin(async move { Ok(candidates.into_iter().map(|(id, _)| (id, 1.0)).collect()) })\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nfn type_context_kind(relation: TypeContextRelation) -> TypeContextKind {\\n    match relation {\\n        TypeContextRelation::SameResolvedType => TypeContextKind::SameResolvedType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::UsesTypeNested => TypeContextKind::UsesTypeNested,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TypeDefinitionImpact => TypeContextKind::TypeDefinitionImpact,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplOfTrait => TypeContextKind::ImplOfTrait,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplSelfType => TypeContextKind::ImplSelfType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::AliasExpansion => TypeContextKind::AliasExpansion,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TraitBound => TypeContextKind::TraitBound,\\n        TypeContextRelation::IteratorSurface => TypeContextKind::IteratorSurface,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ConstGenericAlias => TypeContextKind::ConstGenericAlias,\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// RAG orchestration service.\\n///\\n/// This orchestrates hybrid search by combining:\\n/// - BM25 sparse search served by an in-memory actor\\n/// - Dense vector search served by the HNSW index in the database\\n///\\n/// Notes:\\n/// - BM25 search will gracefully fall back to dense search if the BM25 index is empty,\\n///   ensuring callers do not receive empty results due to indexing lag.\\n/// - Use `hybrid_search` to fuse the results via RRF for robust retrieval.\\n///\\n/// See crate tests for end-to-end examples using a fixture database.\\n#[derive(Debug)]\\npub struct RagService {\\n    db: Arc<Database>,\\n    dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n    bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n    cfg: RagConfig,\\n    io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    type_context_degraded: bool,\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagService {\\n    fn assemble(\\n        db: Arc<Database>,\\n        dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n        bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n        mut cfg: RagConfig,\\n        io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    ) -> Result<Self, RagError> {\\n        let type_context_degraded = Self::apply_type_context_gate(&db, &mut cfg)?;\\n        Ok(Self {\\n            db,\\n            dense_embedder,\\n            bm_embedder,\\n            cfg,\\n            io,\\n            type_context_degraded,\\n        })... [truncated for LLM replay after 200 lines]\",\"end_line\":null,\"exists\":true,\"file_hash\":[3,225,39,9,87,171,21,59,45,239,60,106,148,35,33,124,72,9,183,6,30,13,150,107,95,41,53,154,203,249,220,240],\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/crates/ploke-rag/src/core/mod.rs\",\"llm_replay_max_lines\":200,\"llm_replay_truncated\":true,\"ok\":true,\"start_line\":null,\"truncated\":true}",

142634:      "content": "{\"ok\":true,\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/crates/ploke-rag/src/lib.rs\",\"exists\":true,\"byte_len\":7321,\"start_line\":null,\"end_line\":null,\"truncated\":false,\"content\":\"#![allow(unused_variables, unused_imports, dead_code)]\\n//! ploke-rag — Retrieval and context assembly for the PLOKE workspace\\n//!\\n//! This crate provides the \\\"R\\\" and \\\"A\\\" of RAG for the PLOKE workspace:\\n//! - Retrieval: sparse (BM25) via an in-memory actor and dense (HNSW) via `ploke-db`.\\n//! - Fusion: score normalization, weighted Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF), optional Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR).\\n//! - Context assembly: selecting, trimming, ordering and packaging snippets under a token budget.\\n//!\\n//! The design emphasizes deterministic behavior, strong observability, and strict error handling,\\n//! while integrating with the rest of the PLOKE system (database, IO manager, and embedding backends).\\n//!\\n//! Highlights\\n//! - Async BM25 client wrapper with timeouts, retries/backoff, and strict vs. lenient modes.\\n//! - Dense retrieval across all primary node types using [`ploke_db`]'s HNSW helpers.\\n//! - Pluggable fusion (RRF + MMR) and score normalization utilities.\\n//! - Context assembly with budgeting, deduplication, and stable ordering.\\n//! - Structured tracing for operability (suitable for tests and metrics-ready).\\n//!\\n//! Concurrency and actors\\n//! - BM25 is served by an actor (Tokio task) in `ploke-db` receiving `Bm25Cmd` over `mpsc` and\\n//!   replying via `oneshot` channels. All calls from this crate are enforced with client-side timeouts.\\n//! - Dense retrieval executes against `ploke-db` HNSW via synchronous calls wrapped in async fns.\\n//! - Snippet IO is delegated to `ploke-io`'s actor (if configured) and is used by reranking and\\n//!   by the context assembler.\\n//!\\n//! Error handling\\n//! - Channel send/recv failures map to [`RagError::Channel`].\\n//! - Database/actor errors map to [`RagError::Db`] while preserving inner messages.\\n//! - Embedding pipeline failures map to [`RagError::Embed`].\\n//! - Search state violations (e.g., strict BM25 while uninitialized) map to [`RagError::Search`].\\n//! - A conversion into the workspace error type is provided (`impl From<RagError> for ploke_error::Error`).\\n//!\\n//! Observability\\n//! - The BM25 client wrapper emits fields such as: `bm25_status`, `strict`, `attempts`,\\n//!   `timeout_ms`, `fallback_used`, `bm25_results`, and `dense_results`.\\n//! - Tests can opt-in to tracing; non-test binaries get a minimal, target-less fmt layer by default.\\n//!\\n//! Configuration\\n//! - See [`RagConfig`] to control BM25 timeouts/backoff/strict default, fusion settings,\\n//!   per-type dense search parameters, context assembly policy, tokenizer, and (optional) reranker.\\n//!\\n//! Quickstart\\n//! ```no_run\\n//! use std::sync::Arc;\\n//! use ploke_core::RetrievalScope;\\n//! use ploke_rag::{RagService, RetrievalStrategy, TokenBudget};\\n//! use ploke_embed::runtime::EmbeddingRuntime;\\n//! use ploke_db::{Database};\\n//!\\n//! # async fn doc_example(mut db: Arc<Database>, embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\\n//! // Construct the service (BM25 actor is started internally).\\n//! let rag = RagService::new(db.clone(), embedder.clone())?;\\n//!\\n//! // Sparse-only (lenient): falls back to dense if BM25 is unready/empty.\\n//! let bm25_hits = rag.search_bm25(\\\"how to implement graph nodes\\\", 15, RetrievalScope::LoadedWorkspace).await?;\\n//!\\n//! // Hybrid: concurrent BM25 + dense, fused via weighted RRF.\\n//! let fused = rag.hybrid_search(\\\"module visibility rules\\\", 15, RetrievalScope::LoadedWorkspace).await?;\\n//!\\n//! // Assemble a context for downstream prompting.\\n//! let budget = TokenBudget { max_total: 1024, per_file_max: 512, per_part_max: 256 };\\n//! let ctx = rag.get_context(\\n//!     \\\"module visibility rules\\\",\\n//!     12,\\n//!     &budget,\\n//!     &RetrievalStrategy::Hybrid { rrf: Default::default(), mmr: None },\\n//!     RetrievalScope::LoadedWorkspace,\\n//! ).await?;\\n//! # Ok(()) }\\n//! ```\\n//!\\n//! BM25 lifecycle and persistence\\n//! - This crate re-exports [`Bm25Status`] and provides thin call-throughs for `save` and `load`.\\n//!   Current persistence is a lightweight sidecar (version + doc_count) and `load` triggers a rebuild\\n//!   from the DB in `ploke-db`. Keep API usage stable to benefit from future serialization improvements.\\n//!\\n//! Algorithms\\n//! - RRF: [`rrf_fuse`] implements weighted reciprocal rank fusion with stable UUID tie-breaking.\\n//! - Score normalization: [`normalize_scores`] provides min-max, z-score, and logistic transforms.\\n//! - MMR: [`mmr_select`] performs diversity-aware selection using cosine similarity on normalized vectors.\\n//!\\n//! Threading and performance\\n//! - `RagService` holds `Arc`s to the database handle and embedding processor, and an `mpsc` sender to BM25.\\n//!   Clone or share it behind `Arc` in your application services.\\n//! - BM25 RPCs are local and fast; a client-side timeout guards unexpected stalls (default 250ms) with\\n//!   up to two status/backoff retries in lenient mode.\\n//!\\n//! Stability and versioning\\n//! - Public APIs follow SemVer. The default behavior of existing methods is preserved; strict and persistence\\n//!   features are opt-in. See `crates/ploke-rag/IMPLEMENTATION_NOTES.md` and `plans.md` for cross-crate design.\\n//!\\n//! Re-exports\\n//! - [`Bm25Status`], [`RrfConfig`], [`MmrConfig`], [`Similarity`], [`ScoreNorm`],\\n//!   [`normalize_scores`], [`rrf_fuse`], [`mmr_select`], and context assembly types.\\n//!\\n//! Tips\\n//! - In tests, initialize tracing with your preferred subscriber; the library avoids double-registration.\\n//! - Provide a custom [`TokenCounter`] to align budgeting with your downstream LLM tokenizer.\\n//!\\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\\nuse std::sync::Arc;\\n// use tracing_subscriber::{prelude::__tracing_subscriber_SubscriberExt, util::SubscriberInitExt};\\n\\n#[cfg(not(test))]\\nstatic TRACER_INIT: std::sync::Once = std::sync::Once::new();\\n\\n#[cfg(test)]\\nstatic _TRACE_MARKER: () = ();\\n\\n// #[cfg(not(test))]\\n// fn ensure_tracer_initialized() {\\n//     TRACER_INIT.call_once(|| {\\n//         let fmt_layer = tracing_subscriber::fmt::layer().with_target(false);\\n//         let _ = tracing_subscriber::registry().with(fmt_layer).try_init();\\n//     });\\n// }\\n\\n#[cfg(test)]\\nfn ensure_tracer_initialized() {\\n    // Tests configure tracing themselves; no-op to avoid interfering with test harness.\\n}\\n\\nuse ploke_core::{\\n    EmbeddingData, RetrievalScope,\\n    rag_types::{TypeContextInfo, TypeContextKind},\\n};\\nuse ploke_db::{\\n    Database, DbError, NodeType, SimilarArgs, TypeContextOptions, TypeContextRelation,\\n    TypeContextSeed, TypedEmbedData,\\n    bm25_index::bm25_service::{self, Bm25Cmd},\\n    search_similar_args,\\n};\\nuse ploke_embed::indexer::{EmbeddingProcessor, IndexerTask};\\nuse thiserror::Error;\\nuse tokio::sync::{mpsc, oneshot};\\nuse tokio::time::{Duration, sleep, timeout};\\nuse tracing::{debug, instrument};\\nuse uuid::Uuid;\\n\\npub mod error;\\npub mod fusion;\\npub use error::RagError;\\npub use fusion::{\\n    MmrConfig, RrfConfig, ScoreNorm, Similarity, mmr_select, normalize_scores, rrf_fuse,\\n};\\npub mod context;\\npub use context::{\\n    ApproxCharTokenizer, AssemblyPolicy, Ordering, TokenBudget, TokenCounter, assemble_context,\\n    assemble_context_with_type_context,\\n};\\npub mod core;\\npub use core::{\\n    NoopReranker, RagConfig, RagService, Reranker, RetrievalStrategy, TypeContextConfig,\\n};\\npub use ploke_db::bm25_index::bm25_service::Bm25Status;\\n\\nconst BM25_TIMEOUT_MS: u64 = 250;\\nconst BM25_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS: [u64; 2] = [50, 100];\\n\",\"file_hash\":[81,78,198,14,143,70,69,183,206,169,253,144,252,12,57,236,173,45,53,115,49,250,210,85,65,99,169,152,98,195,120,86]}",

142710:      "content": "{\"byte_len\":35226,\"content\":\"#![allow(missing_docs)]\\n//! Core orchestration: `RagService` and configuration.\\n//!\\n//! [`RagService`] is the primary entry point for performing sparse, dense, and hybrid retrieval,\\n//! as well as assembling a token-budgeted context. It wraps the BM25 actor with timeouts, optional\\n//! retries/backoff (lenient mode), and exposes strict variants and persistence hooks.\\n//!\\n//! Configure behavior via [`RagConfig`], including fusion defaults, dense search parameters, and\\n//! context assembly policy. For diversity or learning-to-rank experiments, plug in a custom [`Reranker`].\\nmod unit_tests;\\nuse super::*;\\nuse ploke_core::rag_types::AssembledContext;\\nuse ploke_embed::indexer::EmbeddingProcessor;\\nuse ploke_embed::runtime::EmbeddingRuntime;\\nuse ploke_io::IoManagerHandle;\\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nuse std::collections::HashSet;\\nuse std::future::Future;\\nuse std::pin::Pin;\\nuse std::sync::Arc;\\nuse tracing::trace;\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub enum RetrievalStrategy {\\n    Dense,\\n    Sparse {\\n        strict: Option<bool>,\\n    },\\n    Hybrid {\\n        rrf: RrfConfig,\\n        mmr: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    },\\n}\\nimpl Default for RetrievalStrategy {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self::Hybrid {\\n            rrf: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr: None,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct SearchParams {\\n    pub ef: usize,\\n    pub radius: f64,\\n    pub max_hits: usize,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for SearchParams {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            ef: 10,\\n            radius: 10.0,\\n            max_hits: 0,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\\npub struct RagConfig {\\n    pub bm25_timeout_ms: u64,\\n    pub bm25_retry_backoff_ms: Vec<u64>,\\n    pub strict_bm25_by_default: bool,\\n    pub rrf_default: RrfConfig,\\n    pub mmr_default: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    pub score_norm: ScoreNorm,\\n    pub search_per_type: HashMap<NodeType, SearchParams>,\\n    pub assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy,\\n    pub token_counter: Arc<dyn TokenCounter>,\\n    pub reranker: Option<Arc<dyn Reranker>>,\\n    pub type_context: TypeContextConfig,\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct TypeContextConfig {\\n    pub enabled: bool,\\n    pub max_seed_hits: usize,\\n    pub max_expanded_hits: usize,\\n    pub score_factor: f32,\\n    pub options: TypeContextOptions,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for TypeContextConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            enabled: cfg!(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\"),\\n            max_seed_hits: 12,\\n            max_expanded_hits: 48,\\n            score_factor: 0.6,\\n            options: TypeContextOptions {\\n                max_distance: 4,\\n                ..TypeContextOptions::default()\\n            },\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for RagConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            bm25_timeout_ms: crate::BM25_TIMEOUT_MS,\\n            bm25_retry_backoff_ms: crate::BM25_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS.to_vec(),\\n            strict_bm25_by_default: false,\\n            rrf_default: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr_default: None,\\n            score_norm: ScoreNorm::default(),\\n            search_per_type: HashMap::new(),\\n            assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy::default(),\\n            token_counter: Arc::new(crate::context::ApproxCharTokenizer),\\n            reranker: None,\\n            type_context: TypeContextConfig::default(),\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagConfig {\\n    pub fn params_for(&self, ty: NodeType) -> SearchParams {\\n        self.search_per_type.get(&ty).copied().unwrap_or_default()\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// Optional async reranker for candidate reordering using snippet texts.\\n#[allow(clippy::type_complexity)]\\npub trait Reranker: Send + Sync + std::fmt::Debug {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>>;\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Default)]\\npub struct NoopReranker;\\n\\nimpl Reranker for NoopReranker {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        _query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>> {\\n        Box::pin(async move { Ok(candidates.into_iter().map(|(id, _)| (id, 1.0)).collect()) })\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nfn type_context_kind(relation: TypeContextRelation) -> TypeContextKind {\\n    match relation {\\n        TypeContextRelation::SameResolvedType => TypeContextKind::SameResolvedType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::UsesTypeNested => TypeContextKind::UsesTypeNested,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TypeDefinitionImpact => TypeContextKind::TypeDefinitionImpact,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplOfTrait => TypeContextKind::ImplOfTrait,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplSelfType => TypeContextKind::ImplSelfType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::AliasExpansion => TypeContextKind::AliasExpansion,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TraitBound => TypeContextKind::TraitBound,\\n        TypeContextRelation::IteratorSurface => TypeContextKind::IteratorSurface,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ConstGenericAlias => TypeContextKind::ConstGenericAlias,\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// RAG orchestration service.\\n///\\n/// This orchestrates hybrid search by combining:\\n/// - BM25 sparse search served by an in-memory actor\\n/// - Dense vector search served by the HNSW index in the database\\n///\\n/// Notes:\\n/// - BM25 search will gracefully fall back to dense search if the BM25 index is empty,\\n///   ensuring callers do not receive empty results due to indexing lag.\\n/// - Use `hybrid_search` to fuse the results via RRF for robust retrieval.\\n///\\n/// See crate tests for end-to-end examples using a fixture database.\\n#[derive(Debug)]\\npub struct RagService {\\n    db: Arc<Database>,\\n    dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n    bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n    cfg: RagConfig,\\n    io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    type_context_degraded: bool,\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagService {\\n    fn assemble(\\n        db: Arc<Database>,\\n        dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n        bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n        mut cfg: RagConfig,\\n        io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    ) -> Result<Self, RagError> {\\n        let type_context_degraded = Self::apply_type_context_gate(&db, &mut cfg)?;\\n        Ok(Self {\\n            db,\\n            dense_embedder,\\n            bm_embedder,\\n            cfg,\\n            io,\\n            type_context_degraded,\\n        })... let (bm25_res, dense_res) = tokio::join!(bm25_fut, dense_fut);\\n\\n        // Propagate BM25 errors as-is.\\n        let bm25_list = bm25_res?;\\n\\n        // Dense errors are mapped to     tracing::trace!(\\\"matches: {matches:?}\\\");\\n        if let Some(namespace) = scope.namespace_filter() {\\n            matches.retain(|doc| {\\n                self.staged_meta\\n                    .get(&doc.id)\\n                    .is_some_and(|meta| meta.namespace == namespace)\\n            });\\n        }\\n        if matches.len() > top_k {\\n            matches.truncate(top_k);\\n }\",\"type_context\":null},{\"id\":\"6297a6a2-7c64-57b1-a925-aac67d039294\",\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/crates/ploke-llm/src/manager/commands.rs\",\"canon_path\":\"crate::manager::commands::search\",\"snippet\":\"mod embedding\\\".to_string()))?;\\n\\n        // Collect results from all node types including methods; pre-allocate to avoid reallocations\\n        let node_types = !search_res.is_empty(),\\n            \\\"Dense search returned no results for '{}'\\\",\\n            search_term\\n        );\\n\\n        let ordered_node_ids: Vec<Uuid> = search_res.iter().map(|(id, _score)| *id).collect();\\n        fetch_and_assert_snippet(db, ordered_node_ids, search_term).await?;\\n        Ok(())\\n    }\",\"type_context\":null},{\"id\":\"72fafdec-a6a7-5bc4-9152-ef37790aa1fb\",\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/xtask/src/commands/pipeline.rs\",\"canon_path\":\"crate::commands::pipeline::search_text\",\"snippet\":\"fn search_text(&self) -> String {\\n        [\\n            Some(self.kind.as_str()),\\n            self.id.as_deref(),\\n            self.pipeline.as_deref(),\\n            self.status.as_deref(),\\n            self.owner_area.as_deref(),\\n            self.summary.as_deref(),\\n            self.stage.as_deref(),\\n            self.path.as_deref(),\\n            self.symbol.as_deref(),\\n            self.role.as_deref(),\\n        ]\\n        .into_iter()\\n        .flatten()\\n        .chain(\\n            self.docs\\n                .iter()\\n                .chain(self.supporting_docs.iter())\\n                .chain(self.tests.iter())\\n                .chain(self.bugs.iter())\\n                .map(String::as_str),\\n        )\\n        .join(\\\" \\\")\\n        .to_lowercase()\\n    }\",\"type_context\":null}]}",

142782:      "content": "{\"byte_len\":35226,\"content\":\"#![allow(missing_docs)]\\n//! Core orchestration: `RagService` and configuration.\\n//!\\n//! [`RagService`] is the primary entry point for performing sparse, dense, and hybrid retrieval,\\n//! as well as assembling a token-budgeted context. It wraps the BM25 actor with timeouts, optional\\n//! retries/backoff (lenient mode), and exposes strict variants and persistence hooks.\\n//!\\n//! Configure behavior via [`RagConfig`], including fusion defaults, dense search parameters, and\\n//! context assembly policy. For diversity or learning-to-rank experiments, plug in a custom [`Reranker`].\\nmod unit_tests;\\nuse super::*;\\nuse ploke_core::rag_types::AssembledContext;\\nuse ploke_embed::indexer::EmbeddingProcessor;\\nuse ploke_embed::runtime::EmbeddingRuntime;\\nuse ploke_io::IoManagerHandle;\\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nuse std::collections::HashSet;\\nuse std::future::Future;\\nuse std::pin::Pin;\\nuse std::sync::Arc;\\nuse tracing::trace;\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub enum RetrievalStrategy {\\n    Dense,\\n    Sparse {\\n        strict: Option<bool>,\\n    },\\n    Hybrid {\\n        rrf: RrfConfig,\\n        mmr: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    },\\n}\\nimpl Default for RetrievalStrategy {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self::Hybrid {\\n            rrf: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr: None,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct SearchParams {\\n    pub ef: usize,\\n    pub radius: f64,\\n    pub max_hits: usize,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for SearchParams {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            ef: 10,\\n            radius: 10.0,\\n            max_hits: 0,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\\npub struct RagConfig {\\n    pub bm25_timeout_ms: u64,\\n    pub bm25_retry_backoff_ms: Vec<u64>,\\n    pub strict_bm25_by_default: bool,\\n    pub rrf_default: RrfConfig,\\n    pub mmr_default: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    pub score_norm: ScoreNorm,\\n    pub search_per_type: HashMap<NodeType, SearchParams>,\\n    pub assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy,\\n    pub token_counter: Arc<dyn TokenCounter>,\\n    pub reranker: Option<Arc<dyn Reranker>>,\\n    pub type_context: TypeContextConfig,\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct TypeContextConfig {\\n    pub enabled: bool,\\n    pub max_seed_hits: usize,\\n    pub max_expanded_hits: usize,\\n    pub score_factor: f32,\\n    pub options: TypeContextOptions,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for TypeContextConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            enabled: cfg!(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\"),\\n            max_seed_hits: 12,\\n            max_expanded_hits: 48,\\n            score_factor: 0.6,\\n            options: TypeContextOptions {\\n                max_distance: 4,\\n                ..TypeContextOptions::default()\\n            },\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for RagConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            bm25_timeout_ms: crate::BM25_TIMEOUT_MS,\\n            bm25_retry_backoff_ms: crate::BM25_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS.to_vec(),\\n            strict_bm25_by_default: false,\\n            rrf_default: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr_default: None,\\n            score_norm: ScoreNorm::default(),\\n            search_per_type: HashMap::new(),\\n            assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy::default(),\\n            token_counter: Arc::new(crate::context::ApproxCharTokenizer),\\n            reranker: None,\\n            type_context: TypeContextConfig::default(),\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagConfig {\\n    pub fn params_for(&self, ty: NodeType) -> SearchParams {\\n        self.search_per_type.get(&ty).copied().unwrap_or_default()\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// Optional async reranker for candidate reordering using snippet texts.\\n#[allow(clippy::type_complexity)]\\npub trait Reranker: Send + Sync + std::fmt::Debug {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>>;\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Default)]\\npub struct NoopReranker;\\n\\nimpl Reranker for NoopReranker {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        _query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>> {\\n        Box::pin(async move { Ok(candidates.into_iter().map(|(id, _)| (id, 1.0)).collect()) })\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nfn type_context_kind(relation: TypeContextRelation) -> TypeContextKind {\\n    match relation {\\n        TypeContextRelation::SameResolvedType => TypeContextKind::SameResolvedType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::UsesTypeNested => TypeContextKind::UsesTypeNested,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TypeDefinitionImpact => TypeContextKind::TypeDefinitionImpact,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplOfTrait => TypeContextKind::ImplOfTrait,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplSelfType => TypeContextKind::ImplSelfType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::AliasExpansion => TypeContextKind::AliasExpansion,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TraitBound => TypeContextKind::TraitBound,\\n        TypeContextRelation::IteratorSurface => TypeContextKind::IteratorSurface,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ConstGenericAlias => TypeContextKind::ConstGenericAlias,\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// RAG orchestration service.\\n///\\n/// This orchestrates hybrid search by combining:\\n/// - BM25 sparse search served by an in-memory actor\\n/// - Dense vector search served by the HNSW index in the database\\n///\\n/// Notes:\\n/// - BM25 search will gracefully fall back to dense search if the BM25 index is empty,\\n///   ensuring callers do not receive empty results due to indexing lag.\\n/// - Use `hybrid_search` to fuse the results via RRF for robust retrieval.\\n///\\n/// See crate tests for end-to-end examples using a fixture database.\\n#[derive(Debug)]\\npub struct RagService {\\n    db: Arc<Database>,\\n    dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n    bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n    cfg: RagConfig,\\n    io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    type_context_degraded: bool,\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagService {\\n    fn assemble(\\n        db: Arc<Database>,\\n        dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n        bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n        mut cfg: RagConfig,\\n        io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    ) -> Result<Self, RagError> {\\n        let type_context_degraded = Self::apply_type_context_gate(&db, &mut cfg)?;\\n        Ok(Self {\\n            db,\\n            dense_embedder,\\n            bm_embedder,\\n            cfg,\\n            io,\\n            type_context_degraded,\\n        })... [truncated for LLM replay after 200 lines]\",\"end_line\":null,\"exists\":true,\"file_hash\":[201,14,255,58,37,64,99,1,221,226,198,215,85,181,21,243,113,176,184,6,168,148,236,14,112,18,42,36,143,57,111,188],\"file_path\":\"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-gated-parent-3g1x3-p3-20260630-174316/prototype1/workspaces/edit-harness/node-ec383aa38762a3d4-r3/crates/ploke-rag/src/core/mod.rs\",\"llm_replay_max_lines\":200,\"llm_replay_truncated\":true,\"ok\":true,\"start_line\":null,\"truncated\":true}",

143034:      "content": "{\"byte_len\":35133,\"content\":\"#![allow(missing_docs)]\\n//! Core orchestration: `RagService` and configuration.\\n//!\\n//! [`RagService`] is the primary entry point for performing sparse, dense, and hybrid retrieval,\\n//! as well as assembling a token-budgeted context. It wraps the BM25 actor with timeouts, optional\\n//! retries/backoff (lenient mode), and exposes strict variants and persistence hooks.\\n//!\\n//! Configure behavior via [`RagConfig`], including fusion defaults, dense search parameters, and\\n//! context assembly policy. For diversity or learning-to-rank experiments, plug in a custom [`Reranker`].\\nmod unit_tests;\\nuse super::*;\\nuse ploke_core::rag_types::AssembledContext;\\nuse ploke_embed::indexer::EmbeddingProcessor;\\nuse ploke_embed::runtime::EmbeddingRuntime;\\nuse ploke_io::IoManagerHandle;\\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nuse std::collections::HashSet;\\nuse std::future::Future;\\nuse std::pin::Pin;\\nuse std::sync::Arc;\\nuse tracing::trace;\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub enum RetrievalStrategy {\\n    Dense,\\n    Sparse {\\n        strict: Option<bool>,\\n    },\\n    Hybrid {\\n        rrf: RrfConfig,\\n        mmr: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    },\\n}\\nimpl Default for RetrievalStrategy {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self::Hybrid {\\n            rrf: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr: None,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct SearchParams {\\n    pub ef: usize,\\n    pub radius: f64,\\n    pub max_hits: usize,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for SearchParams {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            ef: 10,\\n            radius: 10.0,\\n            max_hits: 0,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\\npub struct RagConfig {\\n    pub bm25_timeout_ms: u64,\\n    pub bm25_retry_backoff_ms: Vec<u64>,\\n    pub strict_bm25_by_default: bool,\\n    pub rrf_default: RrfConfig,\\n    pub mmr_default: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    pub score_norm: ScoreNorm,\\n    pub search_per_type: HashMap<NodeType, SearchParams>,\\n    pub assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy,\\n    pub token_counter: Arc<dyn TokenCounter>,\\n    pub reranker: Option<Arc<dyn Reranker>>,\\n    pub type_context: TypeContextConfig,\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct TypeContextConfig {\\n    pub enabled: bool,\\n    pub max_seed_hits: usize,\\n    pub max_expanded_hits: usize,\\n    pub score_factor: f32,\\n    pub options: TypeContextOptions,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for TypeContextConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            enabled: cfg!(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\"),\\n            max_seed_hits: 12,\\n            max_expanded_hits: 48,\\n            score_factor: 0.6,\\n            options: TypeContextOptions {\\n                max_distance: 4,\\n                ..TypeContextOptions::default()\\n            },\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for RagConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            bm25_timeout_ms: crate::BM25_TIMEOUT_MS,\\n            bm25_retry_backoff_ms: crate::BM25_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS.to_vec(),\\n            strict_bm25_by_default: false,\\n            rrf_default: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr_default: None,\\n            score_norm: ScoreNorm::default(),\\n            search_per_type: HashMap::new(),\\n            assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy::default(),\\n            token_counter: Arc::new(crate::context::ApproxCharTokenizer),\\n            reranker: None,\\n            type_context: TypeContextConfig::default(),\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagConfig {\\n    pub fn params_for(&self, ty: NodeType) -> SearchParams {\\n        self.search_per_type.get(&ty).copied().unwrap_or_default()\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// Optional async reranker for candidate reordering using snippet texts.\\n#[allow(clippy::type_complexity)]\\npub trait Reranker: Send + Sync + std::fmt::Debug {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>>;\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Default)]\\npub struct NoopReranker;\\n\\nimpl Reranker for NoopReranker {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        _query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>> {\\n        Box::pin(async move { Ok(candidates.into_iter().map(|(id, _)| (id, 1.0)).collect()) })\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nfn type_context_kind(relation: TypeContextRelation) -> TypeContextKind {\\n    match relation {\\n        TypeContextRelation::SameResolvedType => TypeContextKind::SameResolvedType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::UsesTypeNested => TypeContextKind::UsesTypeNested,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TypeDefinitionImpact => TypeContextKind::TypeDefinitionImpact,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplOfTrait => TypeContextKind::ImplOfTrait,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplSelfType => TypeContextKind::ImplSelfType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::AliasExpansion => TypeContextKind::AliasExpansion,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TraitBound => TypeContextKind::TraitBound,\\n        TypeContextRelation::IteratorSurface => TypeContextKind::IteratorSurface,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ConstGenericAlias => TypeContextKind::ConstGenericAlias,\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// RAG orchestration service.\\n///\\n/// This orchestrates hybrid search by combining:\\n/// - BM25 sparse search served by an in-memory actor\\n/// - Dense vector search served by the HNSW index in the database\\n///\\n/// Notes:\\n/// - BM25 search will gracefully fall back to dense search if the BM25 index is empty,\\n///   ensuring callers do not receive empty results due to indexing lag.\\n/// - Use `hybrid_search` to fuse the results via RRF for robust retrieval.\\n///\\n/// See crate tests for end-to-end examples using a fixture database.\\n#[derive(Debug)]\\npub struct RagService {\\n    db: Arc<Database>,\\n    dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n    bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n    cfg: RagConfig,\\n    io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    type_context_degraded: bool,\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagService {\\n    fn assemble(\\n        db: Arc<Database>,\\n        dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n        bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n        mut cfg: RagConfig,\\n        io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    ) -> Result<Self, RagError> {\\n        let type_context_degraded = Self::apply_type_context_gate(&db, &mut cfg)?;\\n        Ok(Self {\\n            db,\\n            dense_embedder,\\n            bm_embedder,\\n            cfg,\\n            io,\\n            type_context_degraded,\\n        })...
