143058:      "content": "{\"byte_len\":35133,\"content\":\"#![allow(missing_docs)]\\n//! Core orchestration: `RagService` and configuration.\\n//!\\n//! [`RagService`] is the primary entry point for performing sparse, dense, and hybrid retrieval,\\n//! as well as assembling a token-budgeted context. It wraps the BM25 actor with timeouts, optional\\n//! retries/backoff (lenient mode), and exposes strict variants and persistence hooks.\\n//!\\n//! Configure behavior via [`RagConfig`], including fusion defaults, dense search parameters, and\\n//! context assembly policy. For diversity or learning-to-rank experiments, plug in a custom [`Reranker`].\\nmod unit_tests;\\nuse super::*;\\nuse ploke_core::rag_types::AssembledContext;\\nuse ploke_embed::indexer::EmbeddingProcessor;\\nuse ploke_embed::runtime::EmbeddingRuntime;\\nuse ploke_io::IoManagerHandle;\\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nuse std::collections::HashSet;\\nuse std::future::Future;\\nuse std::pin::Pin;\\nuse std::sync::Arc;\\nuse tracing::trace;\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub enum RetrievalStrategy {\\n    Dense,\\n    Sparse {\\n        strict: Option<bool>,\\n    },\\n    Hybrid {\\n        rrf: RrfConfig,\\n        mmr: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    },\\n}\\nimpl Default for RetrievalStrategy {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self::Hybrid {\\n            rrf: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr: None,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct SearchParams {\\n    pub ef: usize,\\n    pub radius: f64,\\n    pub max_hits: usize,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for SearchParams {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            ef: 10,\\n            radius: 10.0,\\n            max_hits: 0,\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\\npub struct RagConfig {\\n    pub bm25_timeout_ms: u64,\\n    pub bm25_retry_backoff_ms: Vec<u64>,\\n    pub strict_bm25_by_default: bool,\\n    pub rrf_default: RrfConfig,\\n    pub mmr_default: Option<MmrConfig>,\\n    pub score_norm: ScoreNorm,\\n    pub search_per_type: HashMap<NodeType, SearchParams>,\\n    pub assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy,\\n    pub token_counter: Arc<dyn TokenCounter>,\\n    pub reranker: Option<Arc<dyn Reranker>>,\\n    pub type_context: TypeContextConfig,\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\\npub struct TypeContextConfig {\\n    pub enabled: bool,\\n    pub max_seed_hits: usize,\\n    pub max_expanded_hits: usize,\\n    pub score_factor: f32,\\n    pub options: TypeContextOptions,\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for TypeContextConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            enabled: cfg!(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\"),\\n            max_seed_hits: 12,\\n            max_expanded_hits: 48,\\n            score_factor: 0.6,\\n            options: TypeContextOptions {\\n                max_distance: 4,\\n                ..TypeContextOptions::default()\\n            },\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl Default for RagConfig {\\n    fn default() -> Self {\\n        Self {\\n            bm25_timeout_ms: crate::BM25_TIMEOUT_MS,\\n            bm25_retry_backoff_ms: crate::BM25_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS.to_vec(),\\n            strict_bm25_by_default: false,\\n            rrf_default: RrfConfig::default(),\\n            mmr_default: None,\\n            score_norm: ScoreNorm::default(),\\n            search_per_type: HashMap::new(),\\n            assembly_policy: AssemblyPolicy::default(),\\n            token_counter: Arc::new(crate::context::ApproxCharTokenizer),\\n            reranker: None,\\n            type_context: TypeContextConfig::default(),\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagConfig {\\n    pub fn params_for(&self, ty: NodeType) -> SearchParams {\\n        self.search_per_type.get(&ty).copied().unwrap_or_default()\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// Optional async reranker for candidate reordering using snippet texts.\\n#[allow(clippy::type_complexity)]\\npub trait Reranker: Send + Sync + std::fmt::Debug {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>>;\\n}\\n\\n#[derive(Debug, Default)]\\npub struct NoopReranker;\\n\\nimpl Reranker for NoopReranker {\\n    fn rerank<'a>(\\n        &'a self,\\n        _query: &'a str,\\n        candidates: Vec<(Uuid, String)>,\\n    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<(Uuid, f32)>, RagError>> + Send + 'a>> {\\n        Box::pin(async move { Ok(candidates.into_iter().map(|(id, _)| (id, 1.0)).collect()) })\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n#[cfg(feature = \\\"typed_type_graph\\\")]\\nfn type_context_kind(relation: TypeContextRelation) -> TypeContextKind {\\n    match relation {\\n        TypeContextRelation::SameResolvedType => TypeContextKind::SameResolvedType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::UsesTypeNested => TypeContextKind::UsesTypeNested,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TypeDefinitionImpact => TypeContextKind::TypeDefinitionImpact,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplOfTrait => TypeContextKind::ImplOfTrait,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ImplSelfType => TypeContextKind::ImplSelfType,\\n        TypeContextRelation::AliasExpansion => TypeContextKind::AliasExpansion,\\n        TypeContextRelation::TraitBound => TypeContextKind::TraitBound,\\n        TypeContextRelation::IteratorSurface => TypeContextKind::IteratorSurface,\\n        TypeContextRelation::ConstGenericAlias => TypeContextKind::ConstGenericAlias,\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n/// RAG orchestration service.\\n///\\n/// This orchestrates hybrid search by combining:\\n/// - BM25 sparse search served by an in-memory actor\\n/// - Dense vector search served by the HNSW index in the database\\n///\\n/// Notes:\\n/// - BM25 search will gracefully fall back to dense search if the BM25 index is empty,\\n///   ensuring callers do not receive empty results due to indexing lag.\\n/// - Use `hybrid_search` to fuse the results via RRF for robust retrieval.\\n///\\n/// See crate tests for end-to-end examples using a fixture database.\\n#[derive(Debug)]\\npub struct RagService {\\n    db: Arc<Database>,\\n    dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n    bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n    cfg: RagConfig,\\n    io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    type_context_degraded: bool,\\n}\\n\\nimpl RagService {\\n    fn assemble(\\n        db: Arc<Database>,\\n        dense_embedder: Arc<EmbeddingRuntime>,\\n        bm_embedder: mpsc::Sender<Bm25Cmd>,\\n        mut cfg: RagConfig,\\n        io: Option<Arc<IoManagerHandle>>,\\n    ) -> Result<Self, RagError> {\\n        let type_context_degraded = Self::apply_type_context_gate(&db, &mut cfg)?;\\n        Ok(Self {\\n            db,\\n            dense_embedder,\\n            bm_embedder,\\n            cfg,\\n            io,\\n            type_context_degraded,\\n        })... Error types for ploke-rag.\\n//!\\n//! [`RagError`] captures channel failures, database/actor errors, embedding failures, and\\n//! search state violations. A conversion into the workspace-wide error type is provided so\\n//! higher layers can uniformly handle failures.\\nuse ploke_db::DbError;\\nuse thiserror::Error;\\n\\n#[derive(Error, Debug)]\\npub enum RagError {\\n    #[error(\\\"Database error: {0}\\\")]\\n    Db(#[from] DbError),\\n\\n    #[error(\\\"Channel error: {0}\\\")]\\n    Channel(String),\\n\\n    #[error(\\\"Embedding error: {0}\\\")]\\n    Embed(String),\\n\\n    #[error(\\\"Search error: {0}\\\")]\\n    Search(String),\\n}\\n\\nimpl From<RagError> for ploke_error::Error {\\n    fn from(value: RagError) -> ploke_error::Error {\\n        match value {\\n            RagError::Db(db_err) => {\\n                ploke_error::Error::Internal(ploke_error::internal::InternalError::CompilerError(\\n                    format!(\\\"DB error: {}\\\", db_err),\\n                ))\\n            }\\n            RagError::Channel(msg) => {\\n                ploke_error::Error::Internal(ploke_error::internal::InternalError::CompilerError(\\n                    format!(\\\"Channel communication error: {}\\\", msg),\\n                ))\\n            }\\n            RagError::Embed(msg) => {\\n                ploke_error::Error::Internal(ploke_error::internal::InternalError::NotImplemented(\\n                    format!(\\\"Embedding error: {}\\\", msg),\\n                ))\\n            }\\n            RagError::Search(msg) => {\\n                ploke_error::Error::Internal(ploke_error::internal::InternalError::NotImplemented(\\n                    format!(\\\"Search error: {}\\\", msg),\\n                ))\\n            }\\n        }\\n    }\\n}\\n\",\"file_hash\":[89,93,15,55,163,241,253,22,222,199,73,182,20,32,103,103,51,195,36,157,132,203,204,176,165,35,194,139,159,91,204,38]}",

json = serde_json::json!({\n            \"kind\": \"materialize_branch\",\n            \"transition_id\": \"33333333-3333-3333-3333-333333333333\",\n            \"phase\": \"before\",\n            \"recorded_at\": 1770000000000i64,\n            \"generation\": 1,\n            \"refs\": refs_json(),\n            \"paths\": paths_json(),\n            \"world\": world_json(),\n            \"hashes\": hashes_json()\n        });\n\n        let record: JournalEntry = serde_json::from_value(json.clone()).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(record).unwrap(), json);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn roundtrips_observe_child_entry() {\n        let json = serde_json::json!({\n            \"kind\": \"observe_child\",\n            \"transition_id\": \"33333333-3333-3333-3333-333333333333\",\n            \"runtime_id\": \"11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111\",\n            \"phase\": \"after\",\n            \"recorded_at\": 1770000000000i64,\n  \"runner_result_path\": \"nodes/node-1/result.json\",\n            \"result\": {\n                \"succeeded\": {\n                    \"evaluation_artifact_path\": \"eval/report.json\",\n                    \"overall_disposition\": \"keep\"\n                }\n            }\n        });\n\n        let record: JournalEntry = serde_json::from_value(json.clone()).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(record).unwrap(), json);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn roundtrips_observe_child_treatment_complete_entry() {\n        let json = serde_json::json!({\n            \"kind\": \"observe_child\",\n            \"transition_id\": \"33333333-3333-3333-3333-333333333333\",\n            \"runtime_id\": \"11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111\",\n            \"phase\": \"after\",\n            \"recorded_at\": 1770000000000i64,\n            \"generation\": 1,\n            \"refs\": refs_json(),\n            \"paths\": paths_json(),\n            \"world\": world_json(),\n            \"child_lifecycle\": \"terminated\",\n            \"runner_result_path\": \"nodes/node-1/result.json\",\n            \"result\": {\n                \"treatment_complete\": {\n                    \"treatment_campaign_id\": \"campaign-treatment-1\"\n                }\n            }\n        });\n\n        let record: JournalEntry = serde_json::from_value(json.clone()).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(record).unwrap(), json);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn roundtrips_child_record_entry() {\n        let json = serde_json::json!({\n            \"kind\": \"child\",\n            \"runtime_id\": \"11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111\",\n            \"recorded_at\": 1770000000000i64,\n            \"generation\": 1,\n            \"refs\": refs_json(),\n            \"paths\": paths_json(),\n            \"pid\": 1234,\n            \"state\": {\n                \"result_written\": {\n                    \"runner_result_path\": \"nodes/node-1/result.json\"\n                }\n            }\n        });\n\n        let record: JournalEntry = serde_json::from_value(json.clone()).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(record).unwrap(), json);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    #[ignore]\n    fn real_campaign_transition_journal_parses_representative_entries() {\n        let path = real_run_root().join(\"transition-journal.jsonl\");\n        let file = fs::File::open(&path).unwrap_or_else(|err| {\n            panic!(\n                \"open real-run journal {} (set PLOKE_RECORDS_REAL_RUN_ROOT to override): {err}\",\n                path.display()\n            )\n        });\n\n        let mut count = 0usize;\n        let mut parent_started = None;\n        let mut observed_child_count = 0usize;\n        for line in BufReader::new(file).lines() {\n            let line = line.expect(\"read journal line\");\n            if line.trim().is_empty() {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            let entry: JournalEntry = serde_json::from_str(&line).expect(\"parse journal line\");\n            count += 1;\n            match entry {\n                JournalEntry::ParentStarted(record) if parent_started.is_none() => {\n                    parent_started = Some(record);\n                }\n                JournalEntry::ObserveChild(_) => observed_child_count += 1,\n                _ => {}\n            }\n        }\n\n        let parent_started = parent_started.expect(\"parent_started entry\");\n        assert!(!parent_started.campaign_id.as_str().is_empty());\n        assert!(!parent_started.parent_identity.node_id.is_empty());\n        assert!(count > 0);\n        assert!(observed_child_count > 0);\n    }\n\n    fn real_run_root() -> PathBuf {\n        std::env::var_os(\"PLOKE_RECORDS_REAL_RUN_ROOT\")\n            .map(PathBuf::from)\n            .unwrap_or_else(|| {\n                PathBuf::from(\n                    \"/home/brasides/.ploke-eval/campaigns/p1-edit-surface-history-long-20260508-1/prototype1\",\n                )\n            })\n    }\n}")

143968:first snippet: Some("/// A composite policy that delegates to multiple policies.\n///\n/// Behavior\n/// - classify: returns the maximum severity among inner policies (defaulting to the error's own severity when empty).\n/// - emit: delegates emission to all inner policies in insertion order.\n///\n/// Example\n/// ```rust,ignore\n/// use ploke_error::{policy::{CombinedPolicy, NoopPolicy}, ErrorPolicy};\n/// let policy = CombinedPolicy::new()\n///     .push(NoopPolicy::default());\n/// // Optionally add feature-gated policies:\n/// // #[cfg(feature = \"tracing\")] let policy = policy.push(ploke_error::policy::TracingPolicy::default());\n/// // #[cfg(feature = \"diagnostic\")] let policy = policy.push(ploke_error::policy::MiettePolicy::default());\n/// ```\n#[derive(Default)]\npub struct CombinedPolicy {\n    policies: Vec<Box<dyn ErrorPolicy>>,\n}")

144071:first snippet: Some("#[cfg(all(feature = \"live_api_tests\", feature = \"flakey_test\"))]\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use reqwest::Client;\n    use std::time::Duration;\n\n    use crate::router_only::openrouter::OpenRouter;\n    use crate::router_only::{Router, openrouter::providers::ProvidersResponse};\n    use crate::utils::test_helpers::default_headers;\n    // use crate::{\n    //     test_harness::{default_headers, openrouter_env},\n    //     user_config::openrouter_url,\n    // };\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    /// Flakey test to help notice when OpenRouter changes their provider list.\n    async fn flakey_openrouter_providers() -> color_eyre::Result<()> {\n        let client = Client::builder()\n            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(5))\n            .default_headers(default_headers())\n            .build()\n            .expect(\"client\");\n        let url = OpenRouter::PROVIDERS_URL;\n        eprintln!(\"url: {}\", url);\n        let api_key = OpenRouter::resolve_api_key()?;\n\n        let resp = client\n            .get(url)\n            .bearer_auth(&api_key)\n            .send()\n            .await\n            .and_then(|r| r.error_for_status())?;\n        let body = resp.json::<serde_json::Value>().await?;\n        {\n            use std::fs;\n            use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};\n            let mut dir = ploke_test_utils::workspace_root();\n            dir.push(\"crates/ploke-tui/data/providers\");\n            fs::create_dir_all(&dir).ok();\n            let ts = SystemTime::now()\n                .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)\n                .unwrap_or_default()\n                .as_secs();\n            let mut path = dir;\n            path.push(format!(\"providers-{}.json\", ts));\n            let f = fs::File::create(&path)?;\n            serde_json::to_writer_pretty(f, &body)?;\n            eprintln!(\"wrote providers JSON to {}\", path.display());\n        }\n        let providers_response: ProvidersResponse = serde_json::from_value(body)?;\n\n        let count_providers = providers_response.data.len();\n        assert_eq!(68, count_providers);\n\n        let count_tos = providers_response\n            .data\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|p| p.privacy_policy_url.is_some())\n            .count();\n        assert_eq!(62, count_tos);\n\n        let count_status_page = providers_response\n            .data\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|p| p.status_page_url.is_some())\n            .count();\n        assert_eq!(24, count_status_page);\n\n        let count_pp = providers_response\n            .data\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|p| p.privacy_policy_url.is_some())\n            .count();\n        assert_eq!(62, count_pp);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}")

count_tos = providers_response\n            .data\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|p| p.privacy_policy_url.is_some())\n            .count();\n        assert_eq!(62, count_tos);\n\n        let count_status_page = providers_response\n            .data\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|p| p.status_page_url.is_some())\n            .count();\n        assert_eq!(24, count_status_page);\n\n        let count_pp = providers_response\n            .data\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|p| p.privacy_policy_url.is_some())\n            .count();\n        assert_eq!(62, count_pp);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}")
144083:first snippet: Some("#[cfg(all(feature = \"live_api_tests\", feature = \"flakey_test\"))]\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use reqwest::Client;\n    use std::time::Duration;\n\n    use crate::router_only::openrouter::OpenRouter;\n    use crate::router_only::{Router, openrouter::providers::ProvidersResponse};\n    use crate::utils::test_helpers::default_headers;\n    // use crate::{\n    //     test_harness::{default_headers, openrouter_env},\n    //     user_config::openrouter_url,\n    // };\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    /// Flakey test to help notice when OpenRouter changes their provider list.\n    async fn flakey_openrouter_providers() -> color_eyre::Result<()> {\n        let client = Client::builder()\n            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(5))\n            .default_headers(default_headers())\n            .build()\n            .expect(\"client\");\n        let url = OpenRouter::PROVIDERS_URL;\n        eprintln!(\"url: {}\", url);\n        let api_key = OpenRouter::resolve_api_key()?;\n\n        let resp = client\n            .get(url)\n            .bearer_auth(&api_key)\n            .send()\n            .await\n            .and_then(|r| r.error_for_status())?;\n        let body = resp.json::<serde_json::Value>().await?;\n        {\n            use std::fs;\n            use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};\n            let mut dir = ploke_test_utils::workspace_root();\n            dir.push(\"crates/ploke-tui/data/providers\");\n            fs::create_dir_all(&dir).ok();\n            let ts = SystemTime::now()\n                .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)\n                .unwrap_or_default()\n                .as_secs();\n            let mut path = dir;\n            path.push(format!(\"providers-{}.json\", ts));\n            let f = fs::File::create(&path)?;\n            serde_json::to_writer_pretty(f, &body)?;\n            eprintln!(\"wrote providers JSON to {}\", path.display());\n        }\n        let providers_response: ProvidersResponse = serde_json::from_value(body)?;\n\n        let count_providers = providers_response.data.len();\n        assert_eq!(68, count_providers);\n\n        let count_tos = providers_response\n            .data\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|p| p.privacy_policy_url.is_some())\n            .count();\n        assert_eq!(62, count_tos);\n\n        let count_status_page = providers_response\n            .data\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|p| p.status_page_url.is_some())\n            .count();\n        assert_eq!(24, count_status_page);\n\n        let count_pp = providers_response\n            .data\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|p| p.privacy_policy_url.is_some())\n            .count();\n        assert_eq!(62, count_pp);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}")

144235:last snippet: Some("/// Immutable read-side graph assembled from one loaded Prototype 1 run.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]\npub struct Graph {\n    /// Scheduler/prototype node forest assembled from typed run records.\n    ///\n    /// This remains available for process/schedule drilldown and future\n    /// step-through projections. The default `ploke-egui` canvas is now the\n    /// borrowed artifact-first `artifact_tree()` projection instead.\n    pub forest: Option<crate::RunForest>,\n    /// Sealed History is the primary ordering and authority spine.\n    pub history: HistoryIndex,\n    pub authority: AuthorityIndex,\n    /// Recoverable checkout identities observed through History and evidence.\n    pub artifacts: ArtifactIndex,\n    /// Concrete hydrated executions observed through actors and passive records.\n    pub runtimes: RuntimeIndex,\n    /// Generative or compositional actions. Evidence may mention these before\n    /// they are promoted to core History facts.\n    pub operations: OperationIndex,\n    /// Selection candidate universes and their set-scoped memberships.\n    pub candidates: CandidateIndex,\n    pub selections: SelectionIndex,\n    pub metrics: MetricIndex,\n    /// Parent-published child plans that carry patch/surface details.\n    pub child_plans: ChildPlanIndex,\n    /// Typed attachments that explain graph objects without replacing History.\n    pub evidence: EvidenceIndex,\n    /// Canonical agent-turn records available for event-level playback drilldown.\n    pub agent_turn_records: AgentTurnRecordSet,\n    pub warnings: Vec<GraphWarning>,\n}")

144316:first snippet: Some("#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_outcome_content_message() {\n        let body = r#\"{\n            \"choices\": [\n                { \"message\": {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": \"Hello world\"} }\n            ]\n        }\"#;\n        let r = parse_chat_outcome(body).unwrap();\n        match r.outcome {\n            ChatStepOutcome::Content {\n                content: Some(c), ..\n            } => assert_eq!(c.as_ref(), \"Hello world\"),\n            _ => panic!(\"expected content\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn recorded_response_tape_replays_provider_envelopes_through_parser() {\n        let response: OpenAiResponse = serde_json::from_str(\n            r#\"{\n                \"id\": \"recorded-1\",\n                \"choices\": [\n                    {\n                        \"index\": 0,\n                        \"finish_reason\": \"stop\",\n                        \"message\": {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": \"from tape\"}\n                    }\n                ],\n                \"created\": 0,\n                \"model\": \"test/model\",\n                \"object\": \"chat.completion\"\n            }\"#,\n        )\n        .expect(\"response envelope\");\n        let mut tape = RecordedResponseTape::new(vec![RecordedResponse::new(0, response)]);\n\n        let step = tape.next_chat_step().expect(\"recorded chat step\");\n\n        match step.outcome {\n            ChatStepOutcome::Content {\n                content: Some(content),\n                ..\n            } => assert_eq!(content.as_ref(), \"from tape\"),\n            other => panic!(\"expected content response, got {other:?}\"),\n        }\n        assert_eq!(step.full_response.id, \"recorded-1\");\n        assert_eq!(tape.remaining(), 0);\n        assert!(tape.next_chat_step().is_err());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_outcome_text_field() {\n        let body = r#\"{\n            \"choices\": [\n                { \"text\": \"Hello text\" }\n            ]\n        }\"#;\n        let r = parse_chat_outcome(body).unwrap();\n        match r.outcome {\n            ChatStepOutcome::Content {\n                content: Some(c), ..\n            } => assert_eq!(c.as_ref(), \"Hello text\"),\n            _ => panic!(\"expected content\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_outcome_preserves_provider_on_success() {\n        let body = r#\"{\n            \"provider\": \"OpenAI\",\n            \"choices\": [\n                { \"message\": {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": \"Hello world\"} }\n            ]\n        }\"#;\n\n        let r = parse_chat_outcome(body).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(\n            r.full_response.provider.as_ref().map(ProviderName::as_str),\n            Some(\"OpenAI\")\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_outcome_malformed_function_call_returns_finish_error() {\n        let body = r#\"{\n            \"id\": \"google-malformed\",\n            \"choices\": [{\n                \"index\": 0,\n                \"finish_reason\": \"malformed_function_call\",\n                \"message\": {\n                    \"role\": \"assistant\",\n                    \"refusal\": \"Malformed function call: print(default_api.apply_code_edit(edits=[...]))\"\n                }\n            }],\n            \"created\": 0,\n            \"model\": \"google/gemini-2.5-flash\",\n            \"object\": \"chat.completion\"\n        }\"#;\n\n        let err = parse_chat_outcome(body).expect_err(\"malformed function call should fail\");\n        match err {\n            LlmError::FinishError {\n                finish_reason, msg, ..\n            } => {\n                assert_eq!(finish_reason, FinishReason::MalformedFunctionCall);\n                assert!(msg.contains(\"default_api.apply_code_edit\"));\n            }\n            other => panic!(\"expected finish error, got {other:?}\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_outcome_unexpected_tool_call_returns_finish_error() {\n        let body = r#\"{\n            \"id\": \"google-unexpected-tool-call\",\n            \"choices\": [{\n                \"index\": 0,\n                \"finish_reason\": \"unexpected_tool_call\",\n                \"message\": {\n                    \"role\": \"assistant\",\n                    \"refusal\": \"Model tried to call an undeclared function: NonSemanticPatchPatches\"\n                }\n            }],\n            \"created\": 0,\n            \"model\": \"google/gemini-2.5-flash\",\n            \"object\": \"chat.completion\"\n        }\"#;\n\n        let err = parse_chat_outcome(body).expect_err(\"unexpected tool call should fail\");\n        match err {\n            LlmError::FinishError {\n                finish_reason, msg, ..\n            } => {\n                assert_eq!(finish_reason, FinishReason::UnexpectedToolCall);\n                assert!(msg.contains(\"undeclared function\"));\n            }\n            other => panic!(\"expected finish error, got {other:?}\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Real refusal text captured from the state6 `gemini-2.5-pro` direct-Google\n    /// incident (`MALFORMED_FUNCTION_CALL` finish reason). The model emitted a\n    /// Python `print(default_api.non_semantic_patch(...))` call whose `diff`\n    /// argument is a multi-line unified diff, which Vertex rejected as a\n    /// malformed function call.\n    ///\n    /// Provenance: `~/.ploke-eval/instances/prototype1/`\n    /// `p1-g25p-direct-protocol-2target-g0g2-1x3-state6-20260610-014509/`\n    /// `BurntSushi__ripgrep-2209/runs/`\n    /// `run-1781081390723-structured-current-policy-9b2c8ea1/agent-turn-summary.json`\n    const MALFORMED_NON_SEMANTIC_PATCH_REFUSAL: &str = include_str!(concat!(\n        env!(\"CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR\"),\n        \"/test_data/google/malformed_non_semantic_patch_refusal.txt\"\n    ));\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_outcome_malformed_non_semantic_patch_multiline_diff_replays_to_finish_error() {\n        // Replays the captured eval-shape Google response (finish_reason\n        // `malformed_function_call` + a multi-line `non_semantic_patch` diff\n        // refusal) through the production parse path. This locks in the\n        // classification fix (commit 3f4d69ea) against the exact state6 payload:\n        // it must surface as a MalformedFunctionCall FinishError, never as an\n        // unknown tool name or repair loop, and the multi-line diff body must\n        // not break parsing.\n        let value = serde_json::json!({\n            \"id\": \"ZSUpar6LGMiFodAP1JvsmQQ\",\n            \"object\": \"chat.completion\",\n            \"created\": 1781081445,\n            \"model\": \"google/gemini-2.5-pro\",\n            \"system_fingerprint\": \"\",\n            \"choices\": [\n                {\n                    \"index\": 0,\n                    \"finish_reason\": \"malformed_function_call\",\n                    \"message\": {\n                        \"role\": \"assistant\",\n                        \"refusal\": MALFORMED_NON_SEMANTIC_PATCH_REFUSAL\n                    }\n                }\n            ],\n            \"usage\": {\n                \"prompt_tokens\": 8665,\n                \"completion_tokens\": 113,\n                \"total_tokens\": 18503\n            }\n        });\n\n        // The finish reason must deserialize to the dedicated variant.\n        let response: OpenAiResponse = serde_json::from_value(value.clone())\n            .expect(\"captured malformed response deserializes\");\n        assert_eq!(\n            response.choices[0].finish_reason,\n            Some(FinishReason::MalformedFunctionCall)\n        );\n\n        // The driver parse path must surface it as a MalformedFunctionCall finish\n        // error, preserving the multi-line diff content in the message.\n        let body = serde_json::to_string(&value).expect(\"serialize captured response body\");\n        let err =\n            parse_chat_outcome(&body).expect_err(\"malformed multi-line patch call should fail\");\n        match err {\n            LlmError::FinishError {\n                finish_reason, msg, ..\n            } => {\n                assert_eq!(finish_reason, FinishReason::MalformedFunctionCall);\n                assert!(\n                    msg.contains(\"default_api.non_semantic_patch\"),\n                    \"expected captured python call in refusal msg\"\n                );\n                assert!(\n                    msg.contains(\"--- a/crates/printer/src/util.rs\")\n                        && msg.contains(\"replace_with_captures_at_kludge\"),\n                    \"expected the multi-line unified diff body to survive parsing\"\n                );\n            }\n            other => panic!(\"expected malformed-function-call finish error, got {other:?}\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_outcome_choice_error_returns_api_error_with_provider_metadata() {\n        let body = r#\"{\n            \"provider\": \"Groq\",\n            \"choices\": [\n                {\n                    \"index\": 0,\n                    \"message\": {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": \"Let me create the fix:\"},\n                    \"error\": {\n                        \"code\": 502,\n                        \"message\": \"Upstream error from Groq: tool call validation failed: parameters for tool cargo did not match schema: errors: [`/command`: value must be one of \\\"test\\\", \\\"check\\\"]\"\n                    }\n                }\n            ]\n        }\"#;\n\n        let err = parse_chat_outcome(body).expect_err(\"choice error should abort parsing\");\n        match err {\n            LlmError::Api {\n                status,\n                api_code,\n                provider_name,\n                provider_slug,\n                error_source,\n                ..\n            } => {\n                assert_eq!(status, 200);\n                assert_eq!(api_code.as_deref(), Some(\"502\"));\n                assert_eq!(provider_name.as_deref(), Some(\"Groq\"));\n                assert_eq!(provider_slug.as_deref(), Some(\"groq\"));\n                assert_eq!(error_source, ApiErrorSource::ChoiceError);\n            }\n            other => panic!(\"expected api error, got {other:?}\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_outcome_skips_errored_choice_when_later_choice_is_valid() {\n        let body = r#\"{\n            \"provider\": \"Groq\",\n            \"choices\": [\n                {\n                    \"index\": 0,\n                    \"error\": {\n                        \"code\": 502,\n                        \"message\": \"bad first choice\"\n                    }\n                },\n                {\n                    \"index\": 1,\n                    \"message\": {\n                        \"role\": \"assistant\",\n                        \"content\": \"usable second choice\"\n                    }\n                }\n            ]\n        }\"#;\n\n        let parsed = parse_chat_outcome(body).expect(\"later valid choice should still be accepted\");\n        match parsed.outcome {\n            ChatStepOutcome::Content {\n                content: Some(content),\n                ..\n            } => assert_eq!(content.as_ref(), \"usable second choice\"),\n            other => panic!(\"expected content outcome, got {other:?}\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_outcome_top_level_error_preserves_provider_metadata() {\n        let body = r#\"{\n            \"provider\": \"Groq\",\n            \"error\": {\n                \"message\": \"No successful provider responses.\",\n                \"code\": 404\n            }\n        }\"#;\n\n        let err = parse_chat_outcome(body).expect_err(\"top-level error should abort parsing\");\n        match err {\n            LlmError::Api {\n                status,\n                api_code,\n                provider_name,\n                provider_slug,\n                error_source,\n                ..\n            } => {\n                assert_eq!(status, 200);\n                assert_eq!(api_code.as_deref(), Some(\"404\"));\n                assert_eq!(provider_name.as_deref(), Some(\"Groq\"));\n                assert_eq!(provider_slug.as_deref(), Some(\"groq\"));\n                assert_eq!(error_source, ApiErrorSource::TopLevelError);\n            }\n            other => panic!(\"expected api error, got {other:?}\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_outcome_top_level_rate_limit_preserves_status_and_api_code_separately() {\n        let body = r#\"{\n            \"provider\": \"Io Net\",\n            \"error\": {\n                \"message\": \"Provider returned error\",\n                \"code\": 429\n            }\n        }\"#;\n\n        let err = parse_chat_outcome(body).expect_err(\"top-level error should abort parsing\");\n        match err {\n            LlmError::Api {\n                status,\n                api_code,\n                provider_name,\n                error_source,\n                ..\n            } => {\n                assert_eq!(status, 200);\n                assert_eq!(api_code.as_deref(), Some(\"429\"));\n                assert_eq!(provider_name.as_deref(), Some(\"Io Net\"));\n                assert_eq!(error_source, ApiErrorSource::TopLevelError);\n            }\n            other => panic!(\"expected api error, got {other:?}\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_outcome_top_level_error_accepts_non_object_error_shape() {\n        let body = r#\"{\n            \"provider\": \"Groq\",\n            \"error\": \"upstream failed\"\n        }\"#;\n\n        let err = parse_chat_outcome(body).expect_err(\"top-level error should abort parsing\");\n        match err {\n            LlmError::Api {\n                status,\n                message,\n                provider_name,\n                error_source,\n                ..\n            } => {\n                assert_eq!(status, 200);\n                assert_eq!(message, \"Unknown provider error\");\n                assert_eq!(provider_name.as_deref(), Some(\"Groq\"));\n                assert_eq!(error_source, ApiErrorSource::TopLevelError);\n            }\n            other => panic!(\"expected api error, got {other:?}\"),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn parse_retry_after_supports_delta_seconds() {\n        let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();\n        headers.insert(reqwest::header::RETRY_AFTER, \"2\".parse().unwrap());\n\n        assert_eq!(parse_retry_after(&headers), Some(Duration::from_secs(2)));\n    }\n\n    /// Config matching the direct-Google retry budget so the schedule tests\n    /// exercise the same parameters production uses for Vertex DSQ 429s.\n    fn google_like_cfg() -> ChatHttpConfig {\n        ChatHttpConfig {\n            initial_backoff: Duration::from_millis(500),\n            max_backoff: Duration::from_secs(8),\n            max_total_elapsed: Some(Duration::from_secs(60)),\n            ..ChatHttpConfig::default()\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn backoff_ceiling_is_monotonic_exponential_and_capped() {\n        let cfg = google_like_cfg();\n        // 500ms * 2^(attempt-1), capped at max_backoff (8s).\n        assert_eq!(backoff_ceiling(&cfg, 1), Duration::from_millis(500));\n        assert_eq!(backoff_ceiling(&cfg, 2), Duration::from_secs(1));\n        assert_eq!(backoff_ceiling(&cfg, 3), Duration::from_secs(2));\n        assert_eq!(backoff_ceiling(&cfg, 4), Duration::from_secs(4));\n        assert_eq!(backoff_ceiling(&cfg, 5), Duration::from_secs(8));\n        // Cap holds and the schedule never decreases.\n        assert_eq!(backoff_ceiling(&cfg, 6), Duration::from_secs(8));\n        let mut previous = Duration::ZERO;\n        for attempt in 1..=8 {\n            let ceiling = backoff_ceiling(&cfg, attempt);\n            assert!(\n                ceiling >= previous,\n                \"ceiling must be monotonic non-decreasing\"\n            );\n            assert!(\n                ceiling <= cfg.max_backoff,\n                \"ceiling must respect max_backoff\"\n            );\n            previous = ceiling;\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn backoff_ceiling_does_not_overflow_for_large_attempts() {\n        let cfg = google_like_cfg();\n        // Saturating shift/mul must not panic and must stay capped.\n        assert_eq!(backoff_ceiling(&cfg, u32::MAX), cfg.max_backoff);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn full_jitter_stays_within_zero_and_ceiling() {\n        let ceiling = Duration::from_secs(8);\n        for _ in 0..10_000 {\n            let sampled = full_jitter(ceiling);\n            assert!(sampled <= ceiling, \"jitter must not exceed the ceiling\");\n        }\n        // Zero ceiling yields zero (no panic, no negative).\n        assert_eq!(full_jitter(Duration::ZERO), Duration::ZERO);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn schedule_jittered_backoff_respects_ceiling_and_remaining_budget() {\n        let cfg = google_like_cfg();\n        // Early in the request: jittered exponential bounded by the attempt ceiling.\n        for _ in 0..1_000 {\n            let scheduled = schedule_retry_backoff(&cfg, 4, None, Duration::from_secs(1))\n                .expect(\"budget remains\");\n            assert!(scheduled <= backoff_ceiling(&cfg, 4));\n        }\n        // Near the end of the budget the sleep is clamped to what is left.\n        let remaining = Duration::from_millis(120);\n        let elapsed = cfg.max_total_elapsed.unwrap() - remaining;\n        for _ in 0..1_000 {\n            let scheduled =\n                schedule_retry_backoff(&cfg, 6, None, elapsed).expect(\"tiny budget remains\");\n            assert!(\n                scheduled <= remaining,\n                \"must not sleep past the remaining budget\"\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn schedule_stops_retrying_once_budget_is_exhausted() {\n        let cfg = google_like_cfg();\n        let budget = cfg.max_total_elapsed.unwrap();\n        assert!(schedule_retry_backoff(&cfg, 3, None, budget).is_none());\n        assert!(schedule_retry_backoff(&cfg, 3, None, budget + Duration::from_secs(5)).is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn schedule_honors_retry_after_within_budget() {\n        let cfg = google_like_cfg();\n        // A server Retry-After larger than max_backoff is honored in full when it\n        // still fits in the remaining budget (it is NOT clamped to max_backoff).\n        assert_eq!(\n            schedule_retry_backoff(\n                &cfg,\n                1,\n                Some(Duration::from_secs(20)),\n                Duration::from_secs(1)\n            ),\n            Some(Duration::from_secs(20))\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn schedule_drops_retry_after_that_exceeds_remaining_budget() {\n        let cfg = google_like_cfg();\n        // Only 5s of budget remains but the server asks for 20s: we cannot honor\n        // it within budget, so we stop retrying instead of waking too early.\n        let elapsed = cfg.max_total_elapsed.unwrap() - Duration::from_secs(5);\n        assert!(schedule_retry_backoff(&cfg, 1, Some(Duration::from_secs(20)), elapsed).is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn schedule_unbudgeted_caps_retry_after_to_max_backoff() {\n        // Legacy (OpenRouter) path: no total budget, Retry-After clamped to\n        // max_backoff and exponential backoff never blocked by a budget.\n        let cfg = ChatHttpConfig {\n            max_backoff: Duration::from_secs(2),\n            max_total_elapsed: None,\n            ..ChatHttpConfig::default()\n        };\n        assert_eq!(\n            schedule_retry_backoff(\n                &cfg,\n                2,\n                Some(Duration::from_secs(9)),\n                Duration::from_secs(120)\n            ),\n            Some(Duration::from_secs(2))\n        );\n        // Without a budget, a retry is always scheduled regardless of elapsed.\n        let scheduled = schedule_retry_backoff(&cfg, 3, None, Duration::from_secs(600))\n            .expect(\"unbudgeted path always schedules\");\n        assert!(scheduled <= cfg.max_backoff);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn retryable_statuses_are_retried_and_fatal_4xx_fail_fast() {\n        let tuning = RetryTuning::default();\n        // Retryable: 429 RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED, 503 UNAVAILABLE, transient 5xx/timeout.\n        for status in [408, 409, 425, 429, 500, 502, 503, 504] {\n            assert!(\n                should_retry_status(status, &tuning),\n                \"status {status} should be retryable\"\n            );\n        }\n        // Fatal 4xx must fail fast (never retried).\n        for status in [400, 401, 403, 404, 422] {\n            assert!(\n                !should_retry_status(status, &tuning),\n                \"status {status} must not be retried\"\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn body_timeout_after_success_status_is_retried() {\n        let tuning = RetryTuning::default();\n        assert!(should_retry_body_failure(\n            200,\n            &HttpBodyFailure::Timeout,\n            1,\n            &tuning\n        ));\n        assert!(should_retry_body_failure(\n            204,\n            &HttpBodyFailure::Timeout,\n            1,\n            &tuning\n        ));\n        assert!(should_retry_body_failure(\n            503,\n            &HttpBodyFailure::Timeout,\n            1,\n            &tuning\n        ));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn body_timeout_retry_limit_caps_retries() {\n        let tuning = RetryTuning {\n            body_timeout_retry_limit: Some(1),\n            ..RetryTuning::default()\n        };\n\n        assert!(should_retry_body_failure(\n            200,\n            &HttpBodyFailure::Timeout,\n            1,\n            &tuning\n        ));\n        assert!(!should_retry_body_failure(\n            200,\n            &HttpBodyFailure::Timeout,\n            2,\n            &tuning\n        ));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn body_decode_failure_is_not_retried() {\n        let tuning = RetryTuning::default();\n        assert!(!should_retry_body_failure(\n            200,\n            &HttpBodyFailure::DecodeFailed,\n            1,\n            &tuning\n        ));\n        assert!(!should_retry_body_failure(\n            503,\n            &HttpBodyFailure::DecodeFailed,\n            1,\n            &tuning\n        ));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn effective_attempt_timeout_clamps_retries_but_not_first_attempt() {\n        let cfg = ChatHttpConfig {\n            attempt_timeout: AttemptTimeout::fixed(Duration::from_secs(300)),\n            max_total_elapsed: Some(Duration::from_secs(60)),\n            ..ChatHttpConfig::default()\n        };\n        // First attempt always keeps the full attempt_timeout, even when the\n        // elapsed time already exceeds the budget (budget < attempt_timeout here).\n        // A normal single attempt must never be truncated by the budget.\n        assert_eq!(\n            effective_attempt_timeout(&cfg, 1, Duration::from_secs(120)),\n            Duration::from_secs(300)\n        );\n        // A retry is clamped to the remaining budget.\n        assert_eq!(\n            effective_attempt_timeout(&cfg, 2, Duration::from_secs(50)),\n            Duration::from_secs(10)\n        );\n        // With most of the budget left, a retry is still capped at the budget\n        // (it can never exceed max_total_elapsed), not the full attempt_timeout.\n        assert_eq!(\n            effective_attempt_timeout(&cfg, 2, Duration::ZERO),\n            Duration::from_secs(60)\n        );\n        // A retry past the budget saturates to zero rather than underflowing.\n        assert_eq!(\n            effective_attempt_timeout(&cfg, 3, Duration::from_secs(75)),\n            Duration::ZERO\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn effective_attempt_timeout_never_clamps_unbudgeted_routers() {\n        // Legacy/OpenRouter path (no total budget): retries keep the full timeout.\n        let cfg = ChatHttpConfig {\n            attempt_timeout: AttemptTimeout::fixed(Duration::from_secs(300)),\n            max_total_elapsed: None,\n            ..ChatHttpConfig::default()\n        };\n        assert_eq!(\n            effective_attempt_timeout(&cfg, 5, Duration::from_secs(10_000)),\n            Duration::from_secs(300)\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn chat_http_default_timeout_uses_llm_timeout_constant() {\n        assert_eq!(\n            ChatHttpConfig::default().attempt_timeout.for_attempt(1),\n            Duration::from_secs(crate::LLM_TIMEOUT_SECS)\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            ChatHttpConfig::default().attempt_timeout.for_attempt(2),\n            Duration::from_secs(crate::LLM_TIMEOUT_SECS)\n        );\n        assert_eq!(ChatHttpConfig::default().max_attempts, 1);\n    }\n}")
